 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
				SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+10, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+55, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(satirbaslangic-2, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)

		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=stok40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						ondalik=2
					else
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					end if
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
					End If
				Else
'mmm
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					End If
				End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						soltutar=sol
						gentutar=sutungenisligi-2
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
'		res=pe1.line(satirpozr, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2, sagmargin-solmargin)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12

	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2+37, sagmargin-solmargin)
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res=pe1.line(satirsonu, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)


	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	Not3 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3(1)
	Not4 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4(1)

	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	
	satirbitis=satirsonu+2
	SatirYuksekligi = 6
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,soltutar-30,6,30,"Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"Ýndirim %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Ara  Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Genel Toplam")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("right")

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis ,soltutar,6,gentutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, IskontoToplami)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,12,gentutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	satiryuksekligi=5
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sevk Adresi :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+2,6,30,"Notlar :")

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+30,6,70,sevkAdresi1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+30,6,70,not1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+2,6,70,not2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+2,6,70,not3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*6,solmargin+2,6,70,not4)


End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	
	

	Grid(0,1) = 48
	Grid(1,1) = 102
	Grid(2,1) = 22
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 35



	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Numarasý"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklamalar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Toplam Tutar"

	

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"


	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, cizgiboyu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla, sevkAdresi1, sevkAdresi2, sevkAdresi3
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	soltutar=0
	gentutar=0
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 67
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 15 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  Sayfaeni, Sayfaboyu,"landscape")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin+2,SolMargin+85,8,100,"LÝKROM FATURA TALEP FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 12
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra+2
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 2, 6,100,"Firma Ünvaný :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,cHadi)
		
		
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(Adres1) & " " & trim(adres2) &" "&trim(adres3))
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi D. ve No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,30,"Sipariþ No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþ Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Ýlgili Kiþi/Dept.,Tel,Fax:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		kkod=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "CNT_KOD", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")

		kAD=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kkod, "")
		kdep=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kkod, "")
		KDEPAD=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",KDEP,-1)
		kTEL=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kkod, "")
		kFAX=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kkod, "")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(kad)& " / "&trim(kdepAD)&" / "&trim(ktel)&" / "&trim(kfax))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Likrom Prof.No.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Müþteri Ref.No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)


'		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.CHSIPNO)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,80,Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"MUSST",0)))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Teklif Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "TARIH", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,40,"Müþt.Sipariþ Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"MUST",0) ))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+6,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+12,solmargin+48,0,sagmargin-solmargin-48)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+18,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+24,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+36,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)		
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+48,45,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+184,8,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin+184,13,0)	
		
		
' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
				SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+10, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+55, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(satirbaslangic-2, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)

		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=stok40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						ondalik=2
					else
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					end if
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
					End If
				Else
'mmm
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					End If
				End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						soltutar=sol
						gentutar=sutungenisligi-2
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
'		res=pe1.line(satirpozr, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2, sagmargin-solmargin)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12

	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2+37, sagmargin-solmargin)
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res=pe1.line(satirsonu, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)


	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	Not3 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3(1)
	Not4 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4(1)

	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	
	satirbitis=satirsonu+2
	SatirYuksekligi = 6
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,soltutar-30,6,30,"Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"Ýndirim %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Ara  Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Genel Toplam")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("right")

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis ,soltutar,6,gentutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, IskontoToplami)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,12,gentutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	satiryuksekligi=5
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sevk Adresi :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+2,6,30,"Notlar :")

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+30,6,70,sevkAdresi1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+30,6,125,not1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+2,6,125,not2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+2,6,125,not3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*6,solmargin+2,6,125,not4)


End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	
	

	Grid(0,1) = 48
	Grid(1,1) = 102
	Grid(2,1) = 22
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 35



	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Numarasý"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklamalar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Toplam Tutar"

	

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"


	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : L2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : *SATIS-03
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Warranty Claim Form
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0



	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 22
 	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15


	satiryuksekligi = 7
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 50
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 10 * satirYuksekligi

	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					'satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
	
		if DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			Degisken = SiraNo + sayfaNo * sayfaSatirSayisi
		elseif DegiskenAdi = "*" then
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)		
		elseif DegiskenAdi = "TESTARIH" then
			tarih=STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo)
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			Degisken=Doc.Strtonordate(tarih)
		elseif DegiskenAdi = "KESIM" then			
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			kesim=STOK40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirNo)
			Degisken =kesim
		elseif DegiskenAdi = "MIKTAR" then
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			miktar=STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
			Degisken =doc.numformat(miktar,18,2)
		elseif DegiskenAdi = "BIRIM" then
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			birim=STOK40T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)
			Degisken =birim

		Else
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		end if
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				kodadi=STOK40T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr-1, Solmargin+62, 10, 40,kodadi)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
			
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

	for i=1 to 8

	next
'dikey çizgiler

	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 13, solmargin+173, 120, 0)

	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+105,gen,0)
	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+125,gen,0)
	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+135,gen,0)
	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+150,gen,0)
	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+35, SolMargin+170,gen,0)

	res = pe1.line(UstMargin+149, SolMargin+210,31,0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.2)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.4)



	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	for i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(ustmargin, solMargin + 200, 5, 20, "Page")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(12,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = ""
	Grid(3,0) = ""
	Grid(4,0) = ""
	Grid(5,0) = ""
	Grid(6,0) = ""
	Grid(7,0) = ""
	Grid(8,0) = ""
	Grid(9,0) = ""
	Grid(10,0) = ""
	Grid(11,0) = ""

	Grid(0,1) = 12
	Grid(1,1) = 25
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 38
	Grid(4,1) = 15
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 15
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 20
	Grid(9,1) = 22
	Grid(10,1) = 25
	Grid(11,1) = 15

	Grid(0,2) = "Item"
	Grid(1,2) = "Part Number"
	Grid(2,2) = "Serial# / Lot #"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = ""
	Grid(5,2) = "Qty"
	Grid(6,2) = "Model Code"
	Grid(7,2) = ""
	Grid(8,2) = ""
	Grid(9,2) = "Disposition Code (3)"
	Grid(10,2) = "Authorization (4)"
	Grid(11,2) = "Date (5)"	

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"
	Grid(11,4) = "C"
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			If i = 7 Then
				ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
				dToplamTutar = Doc.SigDig(ToplamTutar)
				IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
				dIskontoToplami = Doc.SigDig(IskontoToplami)
				NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
				dNetTutar = Doc.SigDig(NetTutar)
				KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVTutari = Doc.SigDig(KDVTutari)
				KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVDahilTutar = Doc.SigDig(KDVDahilTutar)

				Ondalik1 = Ondalik 
				If dIskontoToplami > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dIskontoToplami
				ElseIf dNetTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dNetTutar
				ElseIf dKDVTutari > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVTutari
				ElseIf dKDVDahilTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVDahilTutar
				End If

				If Ondalik1 > 2 Then Ondalik1 = 2
				grid(i,6) = Ondalik1
			End If
		End If
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : L2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : *STOK20-I
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, SolMargin, SagMargin, Ustmargin, AltMargin, FormBaslik, EnAlt
Public AlanSayisi()	, satirYuksekligi
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ToplamEn = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	EnAlt = 0

'***
	FormBaslik = "Üretim Fiþi - Kulanýlan Hammaddeler"
	VPEAdi = FormBaslik
	MustEnter = "KOD"
'***
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	For i = STOK20TI.GetRecCount() To 1 Step -1
		If trim(STOK20TI.GetFieldValue(MustEnter,i)) <> "" Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = i 
			Exit For
		ElseIf i = 1 Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = 1 
		End If
	Next

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297

	res = pe1.Open(0, VPEAdi, SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")

	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 4
	Call Alanlar()

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)

	res = pe1.nopen()
	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(Ustmargin + 2, SolMargin, 15, 55, logo, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "12")
			res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin + 2, SolMargin + 2, 15, 150,FirmaAdi)
		End If
	Else
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 1, SolMargin, 10, 35, logo, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")
			res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin + 1, SolMargin + 2, 15, 150,FirmaAdi)
		End If
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	BBasla = UstMargin + 20
	SatirAra = 6
'*******************
	Blok10 = SolMargin + 2
	Blok11 = SolMargin + 35
	Blok20 = SolMargin + 95
	Blok21 = SolMargin + 121
	Blok30 = SolMargin + 188
	Blok31 = SolMargin + 214

	Genislik10 = Blok11 - Blok10 - 1
	Genislik20 = Blok21 - Blok20 - 1
	Genislik30 = Blok31 - Blok30 - 1

	Genislik11 = Blok20 - Blok11 - 1
	Genislik21 = Blok30 - Blok21 - 1
	Genislik31 = SagMargin - Blok31 - 1

	
	BBasla = 32
	SatirAra = 8
	FisNo = STOK20E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK20E.TARIH(1))
	Onay = STOK20E.ONAYEH1(1)
	FasonMu = STOK20E.FASONNORMAL(1)
	OtomatikRez = STOK20E.MPSSREZ1(1)
	MamulAmbarKodu = STOK20E.AMBCODE(1)
	MamulAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",MamulAmbarKodu,"")  
	ImAmbarKodu = STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE(1)
	ImAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",ImAmbarKodu,"")  		
	YUAmbarKodu = STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE(1)
	YUAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",YUAmbarKodu,"")  				
	NitelikKodu = STOK20E.NITELIK(1)  
	Nitelik = doc.GetGecoValue("STKNIT","AD",NitelikKodu ,-1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "Fiþ No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & trim(FisNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "Onay")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok21, 6, Genislik21, ": " & Onay)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla, Blok30, 6, Genislik30, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok31, 6, Genislik31, ": " & Tarih)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "Mamul Ambar")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & trim(MamulAmbarKodu ) & "  " & Trim(MamulAmbarAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "Ýmalat Ambarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok21, 6, Sagmargin - Blok21 - 1, ": " & trim(ImAmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(ImAmbarAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "Yan Ürün Ambarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & trim(YUAmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(YUAmbarAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "Fason/Normal")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6, Genislik21, ": " & FasonMu)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok30, 6, Genislik30, "Nitelik")		
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok31, 6, Genislik31, ": " & Trim(NitelikKodu) & "  " & trim(Nitelik))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)		

'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 15
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
'		msgbox Baslik
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 1
			j = j + 1
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If
		
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK20TI.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			SN = STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM(satirNo)
			MamulsatirSayisi = STOK20T.GetRecCount()
			For k = 1 To MamulsatirSayisi
				If STOK20T.KARSITRNUM(k) = SN Then
					UrunMiktari = STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR(k)
					UrunBirimi = STOK20T.SF_SF_UNIT(k)
					Exit For
				End If
			Next
			If DegiskenAdi = "_URUNMIKTARI" Then
				Degisken = UrunMiktari
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_URUNBIRIMI" Then
				Degisken = UrunBirimi
			End If
		End If
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'dýþ çerçeve
	If EnAlt = 0 Or EnAlt > SayfaBoyu Then EnAlt = AltMargin 
	EnAlt = EnAlt + 1
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin - 2, SolMargin, EnAlt - UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - SolMargin)
'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, EnAlt - satirBaslangic + 14, 0)
	Next
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 40,5,12,"Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 28,5,27,":  " & i & "  /  " & pe1.GetPageCount())
'		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, SagMargin - 40,5,12,"Tarih")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, SagMargin - 28,5,27,":  " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun()))
	Next
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(11,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,0) = "SERINO"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "KARSITRNUM"
	Grid(7,0) = "KARSICODE"
	Grid(8,0) = "KARSICODE_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(9,0) = "_URUNMIKTARI"
	Grid(10,0) = "_URUNBIRIMI"

	Grid(0,1) = 40 	'"KOD"
	Grid(1,1) = 55		'"KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,1) = 0 '23		'"LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,1) = 0 '25		'"SERINO"
	Grid(4,1) = 30		'"SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,1) = 13		'"SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,1) = 15		'"KARSITRNUM"
	Grid(7,1) = 34		'"KARSICODE"
	Grid(8,1) = 50		'"KARSICODE_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(9,1) = 25		'"_URUNMIKTARI"
	Grid(10,1) = 15	'"_URUNBIRIMI"

	Grid(0,2) = "Hammadde Kodu" 	'"KOD"
	Grid(1,2) = "Hammadde Adý"		'"KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,2) = "Hmd. Lot No"		'"LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,2) = "Hmd. Seri No"		'"SERINO"
	Grid(4,2) = "Harcanan Miktar"		'"SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,2) = "Birim"		'"SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,2) = "Sýra No"		'"KARSITRNUM"
	Grid(7,2) = "Mamul Kodu"		'"KARSICODE"
	Grid(8,2) = "Mamul Adý"		'"KARSICODE_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(9,2) = "Mamul Miktarý"		'"_URUNMIKTARI"
	Grid(10,2) = "Mamul Birimi"	'"_URUNBIRIMI"

	Grid(0,4) = "C" 	'"KOD"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"		'"KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"		'"LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"		'"SERINO"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"		'"SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"		'"SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"		'"KARSITRNUM"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"		'"KARSICODE"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"		'"KARSICODE_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"		'"_URUNMIKTARI"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"	'"_URUNBIRIMI"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK20TI.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : L2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : *STOK60
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : SEVK ÝRSALÝYESÝ - MÜÞTERÝNÝN STOK KODLARIYLA-DENEME-
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu,S60T, sayfasonu
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1

	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 279.4
	sayfaEni = 215.9
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	'res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(46)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 115
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 18
	solmargin = 10
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	sayfasonu= satirbaslangic + satiryuksekligi *24
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	S60T.CopyTable(S60)
	S60T.SafePack
	S60T.GroupBy2("KOD+SERINO")


	satirSayisi = S60T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	chtel_1 = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_TELEFONNO_1(1)
	chtel_2 = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_TELEFONNO_2(1)

	chtel = Trim(chtel_1) + "-" + Trim(chtel_2)

	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if
	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	saat=Doc.Saat(5)

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 96, solmargin + 120, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 96, solmargin + 160, 5, 40, sevktarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 79, solmargin + 160, 5, 40, saat)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 30, solmargin+5, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solMargin+5, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solMargin+5, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solMargin+5, 5, 80, adres3)
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 77, solmargin + 25, 5, 80, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 82, solmargin + 25, 5, 80, vergino)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S60T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonsatir > sayfasonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 0
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		else		

		If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
		end if
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
'		Sol = solMargin + 1 
		Sol = solMargin +4

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
			stokkodu = S60T.KOD(satirno)	
			chkodu=STOK60E.CH_KODU					
			MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
			MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
			SipNo = S60T.SIPNO(satirno)
			ArtNo = S60T.SIPARTNO(satirno)
			MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
			Degisken = S60T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)

'QQ

			Select Case trim(degiskenAdi)
				case "_KOD"
'					degisken = MStokKodu
'					degisken = StokKodu
					degisken = STOK60T.SEVKPAKETLOTNO(SatirNo)
				case "_KOD_STOK00_AD"
					degisken = STOK60T.NOTES(SatirNo)
				case "_MSIPNO"
					degisken = MSipNo
				case else
					Degisken = S60T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
			end select
			if degiskenadi="SEVKPAKETLOTNO" then
					select case trim(doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "GK_1", S60T.KOD(satirno), ""))
								case "D"
										degisken=S60T.SERINO(SAtirno)
								case "B"
										degisken=S60T.SERINO(SAtirno)
									case "SF"
										degisken=S60T.SERINO(SAtirno)
					case Else
						If trim(S60T.NOTES(SAtirno))<>"" then
							degisken=S60T.NOTES(Satirno)
						end if
					end select
				end if

			If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			j = j + 1
			if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" THEN
					Dim Tablo, EvrakKartNo, QStr 
					Tablo = "STOK60T" 
					EvrakKartNo = trim(S60T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(Satirno) )&"                                     "
					evrakkartno=mid(evrakkartno, 1, 30)
					QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
					Set Old = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgfC", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
					For k=1 to Old.GetRowCount() 
						Mesaj = Old.NOTES(k) 
						res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
						satirpozr = satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
						res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin+27, satiryuksekligi, 72,trim(Mesaj)) 
					Next 
				end if

		Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	res = pe1.write(230, solmargin+5, 5, 100, not1)
	res = pe1.write(235, solmargin+5, 5, 100, not2)
	res = pe1.write(240, solmargin+5, 5, 100, not3)


End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "_KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SERINO"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(6,0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0,1) = 23
	Grid(1,1) = 50
	Grid(2,1) = 37
	Grid(3,1) = 13
	Grid(4,1) = 10
	Grid(5,1) = 18
	Grid(6,1) = 18

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"	
	Grid(6,4) = "N"	

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK60T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : L2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : *STOK70
Hangi Tablo için : STOK70
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK70I
Açýklama : SATIÞ FATURASI
Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin, satirbaslangic, satirYuksekligi, satirpozr, sirano, S70T, ST70
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, satirsolmargin, AraToplam, KalanLimit, Grid, satirSonu, charCpi, sonSayfasatirSonu
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, TLOndalik
Sub PrintForm()
		
	GFLSTLOdalik = Doc.Customp_GetS("GFLS_LPT")
	GFLSArr = Split(trim(GFLSTLOdalik), ".")
	TLOndalik = GFLSArr(1)
	satirpozr=0
	charCpi = 17
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
'	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(charCpi)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", 295, 215, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	Ustmargin = 30
	satirSonu = 50 * satirYuksekligi
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 21 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 17
	satirpozr = 0
	satirsolmargin = 10
	AraToplam = 0
	STOK70T.SafePack
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2("XXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
'	S70T.GroupBy2("KOD")

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30

	Set ST70 = Doc.Select2("_STOK70", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST70.GroupBy("IRSALIYENO")

	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()
	sirano = 0
	
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call EnSonSayfa(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Selectfont("arial", 8)
	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")
		
	faturaTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.TARIH)

	IrsNolar = IrsStr(ST70)
	IrsTarihleri = IrsTarih(ST70)
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, CHAdres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres3)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 30, 6, 50, trim(CHKodu))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 50, trim(VergiD))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 65, trim(VergiNo))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 157, 4, 40, trim(IrsNolar))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 110, 4, 20,trim(faturaTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 160, 4, 20,trim(irstarihleri))

		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Müþ.Sip.No : ")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Sip.No : ")

	Set ST701 = Doc.Select2("YYXXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST701.GroupBy("SIPNO")
	for d=1 to st701.getreccount
		sipno=st701.SIPNO(d)
		chsipno=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(chsipno))
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(sipno))

	next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu or (satirNo = S70T.GetRecCount() and sonSatir >= sonSayfasatirSonu) Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic 
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic 
			Else
				satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi 
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 and sirano=1 then 
			res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr , solTutar - 30, satirYuksekligi, 30, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN") 
			res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr, solTutar, satirYuksekligi, GenTutar, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik)) 		
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			satirpozr = satirpozr+satirYuksekligi
			sirano = sirano + 1
		end if
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
				if degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" then
					AraToplam = AraToplam + S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
					solTutar = sol
					GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
				end if
				Degisken = S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
				if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=S70T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK70E.CH_KODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

				if degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					If trim(S70T.NOTES(SAtirno))<>"" then
						degisken=S70T.NOTES(SAtirno)
					end if
				end if
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If		
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)	
				if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" THEN
					Dim Tablo, EvrakKartNo, QStr 
					Tablo = "STOK70T" 
					EvrakKartNo = trim(S70T.ARTNO(Satirno) )&"                                                  "
					evrakkartno=mid(evrakkartno, 1, 30)
					QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
					Set Old = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
					For k=1 to Old.GetRowCount() 
						Mesaj = Old.NOTES(k) 
						res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
						satirpozr = satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
						res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin+25, satiryuksekligi, 72,trim(Mesaj)) 
					Next 
				end if

				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar,5,genTutar,Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik))
End Sub
Sub EnSonSayfa(pe1)	
	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	baslangic1=satirsonu -diptoplamsatirsayisi*satiryuksekligi
			res = pe1.write(baslangic1, 24, satirYuksekligi, 115, "")
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	if trim(STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1))<>"YTL" then
		kur=STOK70T.KUR(1)
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*10
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "KUR :" &STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1)&" "&doc.numformat(kur, 12, 4)&" "&"YTL")
	end if


	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "TESLÝM YERÝ :"& trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi	
	res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3))
	end if

	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "PRICEUNIT" and solTutar > 0 then
			GenPB = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solPB = Sol1
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1-10
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next

	Toplam = STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM
	IskontoTutari = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	IskontoluTutar = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	OTVTutari = STOK70E.TL_VERART1TOPLAMI
	OTVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	OTVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(OTVTutari,IskontoluTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1
'	KDVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,0)

	KDVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTevkifatTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	OdemeSekli = STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1
	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)

	IskontoOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(IskontoTutari,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOndalik = Doc.SigDig(IskontoOrani)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,6,IskontoOndalik)
'***********
	tevkifat=STOK70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI
	Toplam = Doc.NumFormat(Toplam,18,2)	
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,2)
	KDVTevkifati = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatTutari,18,2)
	IskontoluTutar = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoluTutar,18,2)
	IskontoTutari = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoTutari,18,2)
	OTVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(OTVTutari,18,2)
	OTVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(OTVDahilTutar,18,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,2)
	kdvOndalik = Doc.SigDig(KDVOrani)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,6,kdvOndalik)
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatOrani,6, Doc.SigDig(KDVTevkifatOrani))
	OTVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(OTVOrani)
	OTVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(OTVOrani,6,OTVOndalik)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)
	Baslangic = 202
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(Baslangic, solTutar, 5, genTutar + 2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "FATURA TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Toplam)
	if trim(IskontoOrani) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoTutari)


	end if
	if trim(Iskonto2) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani2) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto2)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto3) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani3) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto3)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto4) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani4) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto4)
	end if

	if trim(IskontoTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoluTutar)
	end if

	if trim(OTVTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(OTVOrani) & " ÖTV")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVTutari)

		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÖTV DAHÝL TUTAR")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVDahilTutar)
	end if

	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(KDVOrani) & " KDV")
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTutari)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

if Doc.Round(KDVTevkifatTutari,0) <> 0 then
		AraToplam = KDVTevkifatTutari + STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
		AraToplam = Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam, 18, 2)
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, AraToplam)
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(AraToplam )
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, 60, "TEVKÝF EDÝLEN KDV % "&tevkifat)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTevkifati)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "TAHSÝL EDÝLECEK TUTAR")
	ELSE
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)	
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	end if

	

	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*2, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "Yalnýz#" & Yalniz & "#")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 128, "VADESÝ GEÇEN ALACAKLARDA AYLIK TL'DEN %10 DÖVÝZDE %2,5 VADE FARKI UYGULANIR")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "SON ÖDEME TARÝHÝ :"&" "&doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.VALORTARIH))


	
End Sub
Function IrsStr(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo1 = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)	
		irsaliyeno = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK60E","EXTERNALEVRAKNO", irsaliyeno1, "") 
		if trim(irsaliyeNo1) <> "" then
			Istr = Istr & trim(irsaliyeNo) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Istr) <> "" then
		Istr = left(Istr, len(Istr)-2)
	end if
	IrsStr = Istr
End Function
Function IrsTarih(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		if trim(irsaliyeNo) <> "" then
			Tarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeNo, "")
			Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)
			Itarih = Itarih & trim(Tarih) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Itarih) <> "" then
		Itarih = left(Itarih, len(Itarih)-2)
	end if
	Irstarih = Itarih
End Function
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 23
	Grid(1, 1) = 73
	Grid(2, 1) = 12
	Grid(3, 1) = 8
	Grid(4, 1) = 28
	Grid(5, 1) = 42

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	Grid(5, 4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = S70T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			if Grid(i, 0) = "TL_TUTAR" Then
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik
			Else
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik		
			End if
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Function StringSayisi(Genislik_mm)
	StringSayisi = Doc.Floor(Genislik_mm * charCpi / 25.4)
End Function
Function DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()
	satirSayisi = 6

	

'***********

	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if

	
	DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla = satirSayisi
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
