Makro Kodu : *STOK70-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK70
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK70I
Açýklama : SATIÞ FATURASI- KOD BAZINDA BÝRLEÞTÝRÝLMÝÞ
Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin, satirbaslangic, satirYuksekligi, satirpozr, sirano, S70T, ST70
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, satirsolmargin, AraToplam, KalanLimit, Grid, satirSonu, charCpi, sonSayfasatirSonu
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, TLOndalik
Sub PrintForm()
		
	GFLSTLOdalik = Doc.Customp_GetS("GFLS_LPT")
	GFLSArr = Split(trim(GFLSTLOdalik), ".")
	TLOndalik = GFLSArr(1)
	satirpozr=0
	charCpi = 17
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
'	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(charCpi)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", 295, 215, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	Ustmargin = 30
	satirSonu = 50 * satirYuksekligi
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 21 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 17
	satirpozr = 0
	satirsolmargin = 10
	AraToplam = 0
	STOK70T.SafePack
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2("XXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	S70T.GroupBy2("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S70T.GetRowCount()
		Set ST701 = Doc.Select2("_STOK70ss", "STOK70T", "KOD='" & S70T.KOD(i) &"'", "", -1)
		if ST701.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			S70T.OR_FIYAT(i) = ST701.OR_FIYAT(1)
			S70T.TL_FIYAT(i) = ST701.TL_FIYAT(1)
		End if
	Next

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30

	Set ST70 = Doc.Select2("_STOK70", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST70.GroupBy("IRSALIYENO")

	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()
	sirano = 0
	
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call EnSonSayfa(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Selectfont("arial", 8)
	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")
		
	faturaTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.TARIH)

	IrsNolar = IrsStr(ST70)
	IrsTarihleri = IrsTarih(ST70)
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, CHAdres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres3)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 30, 6, 50, trim(CHKodu))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 50, trim(VergiD))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 65, trim(VergiNo))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 157, 4, 40, trim(IrsNolar))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 110, 4, 20,trim(faturaTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 160, 4, 20,trim(irstarihleri))

		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Müþ.Sip.No : ")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Sip.No : ")

	Set ST701 = Doc.Select2("YYXXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST701.GroupBy("SIPNO")
	for d=1 to st701.getreccount
		sipno=st701.SIPNO(d)
		chsipno=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(chsipno))
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(sipno))

	next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu or (satirNo = S70T.GetRecCount() and sonSatir >= sonSayfasatirSonu) Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 and sirano=1 then 
			res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr , solTutar - 30, satirYuksekligi, 30, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN") 
			res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr, solTutar, satirYuksekligi, GenTutar, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik)) 		
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			satirpozr = satirpozr + satirYuksekligi
			sirano = sirano + 1
		end if
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
				if degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" then
					AraToplam = AraToplam + S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
					solTutar = sol
					GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
				end if
				Degisken = S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=S70T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK70E.CH_KODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

				if degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					If trim(S70T.NOTES(SAtirno))<>"" then
						degisken=S70T.NOTES(SAtirno)
					end if
				end if
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If		
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)	
				if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" THEN
					Dim Tablo, EvrakKartNo, QStr 
					Tablo = "STOK70T" 
					EvrakKartNo = S70T.ARTNO(Satirno) 
					QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
					Set Old = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
					For k=1 to Old.GetRowCount() 
						Mesaj = Old.NOTES(k) 
						res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
						satirpozr = satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
						res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin+25, satiryuksekligi, 72,trim(Mesaj)) 
					Next 
				end if

				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar,5,genTutar,Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik))
End Sub
Sub EnSonSayfa(pe1)	
	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	baslangic1=satirsonu -diptoplamsatirsayisi*satiryuksekligi
			res = pe1.write(baslangic1, 24, satirYuksekligi, 115, "")
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	if trim(STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1))<>"YTL" then
		kur=STOK70T.KUR(1)
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*10
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "KUR :" &STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1)&" "&doc.numformat(kur, 12, 4)&" "&"YTL")
	end if


	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "TESLÝM YERÝ :"& trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3))
	end if

	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "PRICEUNIT" and solTutar > 0 then
			GenPB = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solPB = Sol1
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1-10
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next

	Toplam = STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM
	IskontoTutari = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	IskontoluTutar = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	OTVTutari = STOK70E.TL_VERART1TOPLAMI
	OTVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	OTVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(OTVTutari,IskontoluTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1
	KDVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTevkifatTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	OdemeSekli = STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1
	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)

	IskontoOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(IskontoTutari,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOndalik = Doc.SigDig(IskontoOrani)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,6,IskontoOndalik)
'***********
	tevkifat=STOK70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI
	'tevkifat=Doc.NumFormat(tevkifat,6,2)
	Toplam = Doc.NumFormat(Toplam,18,2)	
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,2)
	KDVTevkifati = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatTutari,18,2)
	IskontoluTutar = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoluTutar,18,2)
	IskontoTutari = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoTutari,18,2)
	OTVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(OTVTutari,18,2)
	OTVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(OTVDahilTutar,18,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,2)
	kdvOndalik = Doc.SigDig(KDVOrani)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,6,kdvOndalik)
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatOrani,6, Doc.SigDig(KDVTevkifatOrani))
	OTVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(OTVOrani)
	OTVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(OTVOrani,6,OTVOndalik)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)
	Baslangic = 202
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(Baslangic, solTutar, 5, genTutar + 2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "FATURA TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Toplam)
	if trim(IskontoOrani) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoTutari)


	end if
	if trim(Iskonto2) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani2) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto2)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto3) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani3) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto3)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto4) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani4) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto4)
	end if

	if trim(IskontoTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoluTutar)
	end if

	if trim(OTVTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(OTVOrani) & " ÖTV")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVTutari)

		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÖTV DAHÝL TUTAR")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVDahilTutar)
	end if

	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(KDVOrani) & " KDV")
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTutari)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

if Doc.Round(KDVTevkifatTutari,0) <> 0 then
		AraToplam = KDVTevkifatTutari + STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
		AraToplam = Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam, 18, 2)
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, AraToplam)
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(AraToplam )
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, 60, "TEVKÝF EDÝLEN KDV % "&tevkifat)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTevkifati)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "TAHSÝL EDÝLECEK TUTAR")
	ELSE
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)	
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	end if

	

	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*2, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "Yalnýz#" & Yalniz & "#")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 128, "VADESÝ GEÇEN ALACAKLARDA AYLIK TL'DEN %10 DÖVÝZDE %2,5 VADE FARKI UYGULANIR")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "SON ÖDEME TARÝHÝ :"&" "&doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.VALORTARIH))


	
End Sub
Function IrsStr(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo1 = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)	
		irsaliyeno = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK60E","EXTERNALEVRAKNO", irsaliyeno1, "") 
		if trim(irsaliyeNo1) <> "" then
			Istr = Istr & trim(irsaliyeNo) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Istr) <> "" then
		Istr = left(Istr, len(Istr)-2)
	end if
	IrsStr = Istr
End Function
Function IrsTarih(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		if trim(irsaliyeNo) <> "" then
			Tarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeNo, "")
			Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)
			Itarih = Itarih & trim(Tarih) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Itarih) <> "" then
		Itarih = left(Itarih, len(Itarih)-2)
	end if
	Irstarih = Itarih
End Function
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 23
	Grid(1, 1) = 73
	Grid(2, 1) = 12
	Grid(3, 1) = 8
	Grid(4, 1) = 28
	Grid(5, 1) = 42

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	Grid(5, 4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = S70T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			if Grid(i, 0) = "TL_TUTAR" Then
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik
			Else
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik		
			End if
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Function StringSayisi(Genislik_mm)
	StringSayisi = Doc.Floor(Genislik_mm * charCpi / 25.4)
End Function
Function DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()
	satirSayisi = 6

	

'***********

	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if

	
	DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla = satirSayisi
End Function

Public ustmargin, solmargin, satirbaslangic, satirYuksekligi, satirpozr, sirano, S70T, ST70
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, satirsolmargin, AraToplam, KalanLimit, Grid, satirSonu, charCpi, sonSayfasatirSonu
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, TLOndalik
Sub PrintForm()
		
	GFLSTLOdalik = Doc.Customp_GetS("GFLS_LPT")
	GFLSArr = Split(trim(GFLSTLOdalik), ".")
	TLOndalik = GFLSArr(1)
	satirpozr=0
	charCpi = 17
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
'	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
'	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(charCpi)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", 295, 215, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	Ustmargin = 30
	satirSonu = 50 * satirYuksekligi
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 21 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 17
	satirpozr = 0
	satirsolmargin = 10
	AraToplam = 0
	STOK70T.SafePack
	Set S70T = Doc.Select2("XXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
'	S70T.GroupBy2("KOD")

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30

	Set ST70 = Doc.Select2("_STOK70", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST70.GroupBy("IRSALIYENO")

	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()
	sirano = 0
	
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call EnSonSayfa(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Selectfont("arial", 8)
	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")
		
	faturaTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.TARIH)

	IrsNolar = IrsStr(ST70)
	IrsTarihleri = IrsTarih(ST70)
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+3, 5, 95, CHAdres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres3)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 30, 6, 50, trim(CHKodu))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 12 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 50, trim(VergiD))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 26, 6, 65, trim(VergiNo))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 13 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 157, 4, 40, trim(IrsNolar))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 110, 4, 20,trim(faturaTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 160, 4, 20,trim(irstarihleri))

		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Müþ.Sip.No : ")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 25, 6, 65, "Sip.No : ")

	Set ST701 = Doc.Select2("YYXXX", "STOK70T", "", "", -1)
	ST701.GroupBy("SIPNO")
	for d=1 to st701.getreccount
		sipno=st701.SIPNO(d)
		chsipno=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 15.5* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(chsipno))
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 17.2* satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 40+d*12, 6, 16, trim(sipno))

	next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu or (satirNo = S70T.GetRecCount() and sonSatir >= sonSayfasatirSonu) Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 and sirano=1 then 
			res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr , solTutar - 30, satirYuksekligi, 30, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN") 
			res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res= pe1.write(satirpozr, solTutar, satirYuksekligi, GenTutar, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik)) 		
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			satirpozr = satirpozr + satirYuksekligi
			sirano = sirano + 1
		end if
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
				if degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" then
					AraToplam = AraToplam + S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
					solTutar = sol
					GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
				end if
				Degisken = S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
				if degiskenadi="KOD" then
					stokkodu=S70T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK70E.CH_KODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

				if degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					If trim(S70T.NOTES(SAtirno))<>"" then
						degisken=S70T.NOTES(SAtirno)
					end if
				end if
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If		
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi*2, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)	
				if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" THEN
					Dim Tablo, EvrakKartNo, QStr 
					Tablo = "STOK70T" 
					EvrakKartNo = trim(S70T.ARTNO(Satirno) )&"                                                  "
					QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
					Set Old = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
					For k=1 to Old.GetRowCount() 
						Mesaj = Old.NOTES(k) 
						res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
						satirpozr = satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
						res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin+25, satiryuksekligi, 72,trim(Mesaj)) 
					Next 
				end if

				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar,5,genTutar,Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,TLOndalik))
End Sub
Sub EnSonSayfa(pe1)	
	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	baslangic1=satirsonu -diptoplamsatirsayisi*satiryuksekligi
			res = pe1.write(baslangic1, 24, satirYuksekligi, 115, "")
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
if trim(STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1))<>"YTL" then
		kur=STOK70T.KUR(1)
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*10
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "KUR :" &STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1)&" "&doc.numformat(kur, 12, 4)&" "&"YTL")
	end if


	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)<>"" Then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, "TESLÝM YERÝ :"& trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2))
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.write(satirPozr, solmargin, satirYuksekligi, 115, trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3))
	end if

	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "PRICEUNIT" and solTutar > 0 then
			GenPB = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solPB = Sol1
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next

	Toplam = STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM
	IskontoTutari = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	IskontoluTutar = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	OTVTutari = STOK70E.TL_VERART1TOPLAMI
	OTVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	OTVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(OTVTutari,IskontoluTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KESINTI1
	KDVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTevkifatTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	OdemeSekli = STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1
	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)

	IskontoOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(IskontoTutari,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOndalik = Doc.SigDig(IskontoOrani)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,6,IskontoOndalik)
'***********
	tevkifat=STOK70E.TEVKIFAT_ORANI
'	tevkifat=Doc.NumFormat(tevkifat,6,2)
	Toplam = Doc.NumFormat(Toplam,18,2)	
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,2)
	KDVTevkifati = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatTutari,18,2)
	IskontoluTutar = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoluTutar,18,2)
	IskontoTutari = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoTutari,18,2)
	OTVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(OTVTutari,18,2)
	OTVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(OTVDahilTutar,18,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,2)
	kdvOndalik = Doc.SigDig(KDVOrani)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,6,kdvOndalik)
	KDVTevkifatOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTevkifatOrani,6, Doc.SigDig(KDVTevkifatOrani))
	OTVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(OTVOrani)
	OTVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(OTVOrani,6,OTVOndalik)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)
	Baslangic = 202
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(Baslangic, solTutar, 5, genTutar + 2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "FATURA TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Toplam)
	if trim(IskontoOrani) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoTutari )
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto2) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani2) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto2)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto3) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani3) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto3)
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto4) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(IskontoOrani4) & " ÝSKONTO")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, Iskonto4)
	end if

	if trim(IskontoTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, IskontoluTutar)
	end if

	if trim(OTVTutari) <> "" then
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(OTVOrani) & " ÖTV")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVTutari)

		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ÖTV DAHÝL TUTAR")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, OTVDahilTutar)
	end if

	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "% " & trim(KDVOrani) & " KDV")
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTutari)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))

if Doc.Round(KDVTevkifatTutari,0) <> 0 then
		AraToplam = KDVTevkifatTutari + STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
		AraToplam = Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam, 18, 2)
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "ARA TOPLAM")
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, AraToplam)
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(AraToplam )
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, 60, "TEVKÝF EDÝLEN KDV % "&tevkifat)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVTevkifati)
		res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"-"))
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
		res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "TAHSÝL EDÝLECEK TUTAR")
	ELSE
		Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(KDVDahilTutar)	
		res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, 5, genFiyat, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	end if


	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, genTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, genTutar+2, String(StringSayisi(genTutar),"="))
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi*2, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "Yalnýz#" & Yalniz & "#")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 128, "VADESÝ GEÇEN ALACAKLARDA AYLIK TL'DEN %10 DÖVÝZDE %2,5 VADE FARKI UYGULANIR")
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin+3, 5, 120, "SON ÖDEME TARÝHÝ :"&" "&doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.VALORTARIH))


	
End Sub
Function IrsStr(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo1 = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)	
		irsaliyeno = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK60E","EXTERNALEVRAKNO", irsaliyeno1, "") 
		if trim(irsaliyeNo1) <> "" then
			Istr = Istr & trim(irsaliyeNo) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Istr) <> "" then
		Istr = left(Istr, len(Istr)-2)
	end if
	IrsStr = Istr
End Function
Function IrsTarih(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		if trim(irsaliyeNo) <> "" then
			Tarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeNo, "")
			Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)
			Itarih = Itarih & trim(Tarih) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Itarih) <> "" then
		Itarih = left(Itarih, len(Itarih)-2)
	end if
	Irstarih = Itarih
End Function
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = ""
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 23
	Grid(1, 1) = 73
	Grid(2, 1) = 12
	Grid(3, 1) = 8
	Grid(4, 1) = 28
	Grid(5, 1) = 42

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	Grid(5, 4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = S70T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			if Grid(i, 0) = "TL_TUTAR" Then
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik
			Else
				Grid(i, 6) = TLOndalik		
			End if
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Function StringSayisi(Genislik_mm)
	StringSayisi = Doc.Floor(Genislik_mm * charCpi / 25.4)
End Function
Function DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()
	satirSayisi = 6

	

'***********

	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_1) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_2) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(STOK70E.S60_SEVKADRESI_3) <> "" then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if

	
	DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla = satirSayisi
End Function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, sira

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	iskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next
	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****LOGO OKU
	
		
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))
res=pe1.nopen
					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))

		End Select
res=pe1.nopen
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)

		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		kontaktpozisyon=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")
		FAX=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kontakt, "")

		adr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		adr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_3", kontakt, "")

		Eadr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		Eadr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		Eadr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "ADR_3", kontakt, "")

		kontaktpozisyon_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",kontaktpozisyon,-1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHtel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHfax = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		
			
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		satirara=5
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 6,100, chadi)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)

		Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
		EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)
 
		QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
		Set Old1 = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
		
		satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom
	
		For  c = 1 To old1.getreccount
			Mesaj = Trim(Old1.NOTES(c))
			Mesaj1 = mesaj1 & mesaj

		Next
					mesaj1=replace(trim(mesaj1),"-"," ") 
		k=len(mesaj1)/100
	if k < 1 then
		k=1
		end if
	k=Doc.Ceil(k)
	res=pe1.setalign("justified")

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5*k,SagMargin - solmargin1-10 ,Mesaj1)	
			
			satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom




		

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiAlan_ad = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","AD",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		NAME2 = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","NAME2",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		EMAIL = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","EMAIL",SiparisiAlan,"") 

		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,"Saygýlarýmýzla,")		
		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,siparisialan_AD)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,naME2)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,EMAIL)	
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube:Beytepe Mah.1725 Sokak. No:12 06800 Beytepe / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")




		res=pe1.pagebreak
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		solmargin=10

				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res=pe1.nopen
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Solmargin, 30,sagmargin-solmargin-10,logo,114)
		

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		TeklifNo = TEKL40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		ProjeAdi = TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Sadres1=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1
		Sadres2=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_2
		Sadres3=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_3
		SAD=TEKL40E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = TEKL40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+38
		SatirAra = 5

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,SAD)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,EAdr1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,EAdr2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,EAdr3)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"From")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 28, 6,85,":  " & Siparisialan)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		'res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 2, 6,26,"To")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 28, 6,30,":  " & trim(Siparisiveren))

		

			Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24


		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Date")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,43,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


' Sütun basliklari
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)

			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		Not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(Satirno)
		
		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1, satirno)
		End If
if trim(not1) <> "" then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If

		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+3
		Else
	If satirNo = 1 Then
				if trim(not1) <> "" then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+2
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If			
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
					Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				elseif degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=TEKL40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If
					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				

				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next		

		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom

End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
if pe1.getbottom+20 > satirsonu then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+1, SolMargin+50 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

end sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, Satirno)


'*************
	Ondalik = 2
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"SUB - TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "SPECIAL DISCOUNT")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	geneltoplam=0
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPTIONS : (will be provided locally)")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_Toplam_Yazdir (pe1)
	Ondalik = 2
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"SUB - TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "SPECIAL DISCOUNT")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTOP ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))
	Todeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"ODEME",0))
	Masraf=(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"MASRAF",0))
	if masraf > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "EXPENSES")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(masraf, 20, ondalik))
	end if
	if trim(Todeme)<>"" then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, TODEME)
	Else
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, ODEME)
	end if
res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat((TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM+masraf), 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	

	
	

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)

'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)

SatirBitis = SatirSonu

'dikey çizgiler

		'res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, sonsatir2-(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3), 0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, cizgiboyu,0)

	Next
'dis çerçeve

' notlar mesajlar

	if trim(Tvalidity)<>"" then

		end if
	end if
if trim(Tdelivery)<>"" then

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Delivery")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(TEKL40T.PRICEUNIT(1))

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "ITEM"
	Grid(1,2) = "CAT.NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "DESCRIPTION"
	Grid(3,2) = "QTY"
	Grid(4,2) = "UNIT"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TOTAL"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 12
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 75
	Grid(3,1) = 20
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Page")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, sira
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar, solad, solmargin1
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n, toplam, iskontotoplam, geneltoplam, blok2, blok21, logo, logo1, gk1
Public xCizgi(), chkodu, sonsatir2, logo_lik, adr1, adr2, adr3

Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	iskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0

	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20
	iskontotoplam=0
	toplam=0
geneltoplam=0

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next

	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****logo
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))

					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))
		End Select
		res=pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")
FAX=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kontakt, "")


		kontaktpozisyon=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kontakt, "")
		kontaktpozisyon_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",kontaktpozisyon,-1)
		adr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		adr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_3", kontakt, "")


		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHtel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHfax = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		

		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		satirara=5
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))



		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 6,100, chadi)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)

		Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
		EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)
 
		QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
		Set Old1 = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
		
		satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom
	
		For  c = 1 To old1.getreccount
			Mesaj = trim(Old1.NOTES(c))
				'If  trim(old1.KOD(c)) = "" Then 
					s=s+1
					sayi=doc.fmod(s, 2)
					mesaj2=mesaj2&" "&mesaj

					if sayi=0 then
						satirpozr1=satirpozr1+SatirYuksekligi
						res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,Mesaj2)	
						mesaj2=""	
					else
						if s=old1.getreccount then
							satirpozr1=satirpozr1+SatirYuksekligi
							res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,Mesaj2)
						end if	
					end if

		Next
		
		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiAlan_ad = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","AD",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		NAME2 = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","NAME2",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		EMAIL = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","EMAIL",SiparisiAlan,"") 

		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,"Saygýlarýmýzla,")		
		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,siparisialan_AD)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,naME2)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,EMAIL)	
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Atatürk Cad. No:82 Sýtkýbey Plaza Kat:6 Daire:20 34736 Kozyataðý-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 368 24 74 pbx Fax: 0216 411 67 95")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Boðaz Sokak No:30/6 06700 Kavaklýdere-Gaziosmanpaþa / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")




		res=pe1.pagebreak
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		solmargin=10

				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res=pe1.nopen()

		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Solmargin, 30,sagmargin-solmargin-10,logo_Lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
'*******************

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		TeklifNo = TEKL40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		ProjeAdi = TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Sadres1=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1
		Sadres2=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_2
		Sadres3=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_3
		SAD=TEKL40E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = TEKL40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+37.5
		SatirAra = 5

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,chadi)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr3)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"From")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 28, 6,85,":  " & Siparisialan)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		'res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 2, 6,26,"To")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 28, 6,30,":  " & trim(Siparisiveren))



			Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,43,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)


' Sütun basliklari
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 11, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(satirno)

		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If
		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				if refnum1 > 0 then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				Else
					Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				End If
					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom
End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, not1, satirno)
	Ondalik = 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
if satirno<> 1 then
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	geneltoplam=0
end if
if pe1.getbottom+5 > satirsonu then
CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)

	res=pe1.pagebreak
Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	if trim(not1)="" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPSÝYONLAR")
	else
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, 58 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
		res=pe1.setbold(1)

	end if
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_Toplam_Yazdir (pe1)
	Ondalik = 2
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTOP ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	'chkodu1=Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",gk1,-1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "Fiyatlarýmýza KDV Dahil Deðildir.")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, doc.numtotext(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM))
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)

SatirBitis = SatirSonu

'dis çerçeve

' notlar mesajlar
	Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
	EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)

	QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'"

	res=pe1.setbold(1)

		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)

		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

if trim(tdelivery)<>"" then

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(TEKL40T.PRICEUNIT(1))

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA #"
	Grid(1,2) = "KATALOG NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "ADET"
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 76
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaeni=210
	sayfiskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0
aEni = 210
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next

	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****logo
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))

					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))
		End Select
		res=pe1.nopen()

		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)

		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")

		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))

		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Beytepe Mah. 1725. Sokak No:12 06800 Beytepe / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		solmargin=10

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)

		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Sadres1=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1
		Sadres2=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_2

		SiparisiVeren = TEKL40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
