		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr3)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"From")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 28, 6,85,":  " & Siparisialan)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		'res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 2, 6,26,"To")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 28, 6,30,":  " & trim(Siparisiveren))



			Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,43,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)


' Sütun basliklari
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 9, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		Not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(Satirno)
		
		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1, satirno)
		End If
if trim(not1) <> "" then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If
		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+2
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				if trim(not1) <> "" then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+2
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				elseif degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=TEKL40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom
End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
if pe1.getbottom+20 > satirsonu then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+1, SolMargin+50 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

end sub

Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, satirno)
	Ondalik = 2
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
if satirno<> 1 then

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
		isk=Doc.div(iskontotoplam, toplam)*100

	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO %"&doc.numformat_nlz(isk,10,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	geneltoplam=0
end if
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPSÝYONLAR")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_Toplam_Yazdir (pe1)
	Ondalik = 2
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
		isk=Doc.div(iskontotoplam, toplam)*100

	TOPLAM=0
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO %"&doc.numformat_nlz(isk,10,2))

		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTOP ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	'chkodu1=Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",gk1,-1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "Fiyatlarýmýza KDV Dahil Deðildir.")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, doc.numtotext(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM))
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirbaslangic + satiryuksekligi*7
'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
	'	res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, sonsatir2-(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3), 0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, cizgiboyu, 0)

	Next
'dis çerçeve
	

	res = pe1.box(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin, cizgiboyu, SagMargin - SolMargin)


' notlar mesajlar
	Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
	EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)

	QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
	Set Old = Doc.Select1("_GNMESGGG", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 

		satirpozr=satirsonu
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)

	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	end if

		

	Tvalidity=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"VALIDITY",0))
	Tpayment=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"PAYMENT",0))
	Twarranty=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"WARRANTY",0))
	TDelivery=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"DELIVERY",0))
	if trim(Tvalidity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+4.5*satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tvalidity)
	Else
		if trim(validity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &validity)
		end if
	end if
if trim(tdelivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tdelivery)
	Else
		if trim(delivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tdelivery)
		end if
	end if

	if trim(tpayment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tPayment)
	else
		if trim(payment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Payment)
		end if
	end if
	
	if trim(twarranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &twarranty)
	else
		if trim(warranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &warranty)
		end if

	end if
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 6)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("center")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Boðaz Sokak No:30/6 06700 Kavaklýdere-Gaziosmanpaþa / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	
			res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(TEKL40T.PRICEUNIT(1))

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA #"
	Grid(1,2) = "KATALOG NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "ADET"
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 76
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, sira
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar, solad, solmargin1,sol
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n, toplam, iskontotoplam, geneltoplam, blok2, blok21, logo, logo1, gk1

Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	iskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0

	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20
	iskontotoplam=0
	toplam=0
geneltoplam=0

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next

	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi- " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO(1)),SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")



	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****logo
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))

					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))
		End Select
		res=pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")
FAX=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kontakt, "")


		kontaktpozisyon=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kontakt, "")
		kontaktpozisyon_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",kontaktpozisyon,-1)
		adr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		adr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_3", kontakt, "")


		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)

		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)

		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHtel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHfax = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1)

		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))



		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)
bos = ""	
		if trim(CHAdi2) = bos then
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 6,100, chadi)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)
		Else
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 10,100, chadi)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2+4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3+4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4+4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5+4, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
			res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6+4, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)
		End if

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)
		Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
		EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)
 
		QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
		Set Old1 = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
		
		satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom
	
		For  c = 1 To old1.getreccount
			Mesaj = Trim(Old1.NOTES(c))
			Mesaj1 = mesaj1 & mesaj

		Next
					mesaj1=replace(trim(mesaj1),"-"," ") 
		k=len(mesaj1)/100
	if k < 1 then
		k=1
	end if
	k=Doc.Ceil(k)
	res=pe1.setalign("justified")

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5*k,SagMargin - solmargin1-10 ,Mesaj1)	
			
			satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom

		SiparisiAlan_ad = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","AD",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		NAME2 = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","NAME2",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		EMAIL = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","EMAIL",SiparisiAlan,"") 

		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,"Saygýlarýmýzla,")		
		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,siparisialan_AD)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,naME2)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,EMAIL)	
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Beytepe Mah. 1725. Sokak. No: 12 06800 Beytepe  / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		solmargin=10

				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res=pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Solmargin, 30,sagmargin-solmargin-10,logo_Lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)

'*******************

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		TeklifNo = TEKL40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		ProjeAdi = TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
'		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Sadres1=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1
		Sadres2=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_2
		Sadres3=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_3
		SAD=TEKL40E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = TEKL40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+37.5
		SatirAra = 5

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

bos = ""	
		if trim(CHAdi2) = bos then
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,chadi)
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr2)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr3)
		Else
'			res = pe1.Write(BBasla-4, SolMargin + 2, 10,100,chadi)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla-4, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,chadi)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla-4 + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,chadi2)
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	 		res = pe1.Write(BBasla -4 + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla -4 + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr2)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla -4 + 4 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr3)
		End if

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"From")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 28, 6,85,":  " & Siparisialan)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		'res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 2, 6,26,"To")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 28, 6,30,":  " & trim(Siparisiveren))


			Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

' Sütun basliklari
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 9, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
			res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		Not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(Satirno)
		
		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1,NOT1, satirno)
		End If
		if trim(not1) <> "" then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If
		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+3
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				if trim(not1) <> "" then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+2
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If	
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
									Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				elseif degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=TEKL40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.OR_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.OR_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom
End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
	if pe1.getbottom > satirsonu-20 then
		CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
		res=pe1.pagebreak
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	end if

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+1, SolMargin+50 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

end sub

Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, not1, satirno)
	Ondalik = 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
if satirno<> 1 then

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	geneltoplam=0
end if

if pe1.getbottom > satirsonu-20 then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if


	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(not1)="" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, 60 , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPSÝYONLAR")
	else
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, 60 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)

	end if
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_Toplam_Yazdir (pe1)
	Ondalik = 2
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
If Iskontotoplam > 0 then

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
end if
	TOPLAM=0
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTOP ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	'chkodu1=Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",gk1,-1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "Fiyatlarýmýza KDV Dahil Deðildir.")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, doc.numtotext(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM))
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirbaslangic + satiryuksekligi*7
'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
	'	res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, sonsatir2-(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3), 0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, cizgiboyu, 0)

	Next
'dis çerçeve
	

	res = pe1.box(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin, cizgiboyu, SagMargin - SolMargin)


' notlar mesajlar
	Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
	EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)

	QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
	Set Old = Doc.Select1("_GNMESGGG", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 

		satirpozr=satirsonu
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)

	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	end if

	'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next
	
	Tvalidity=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"VALIDITY",0))
	Tpayment=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"PAYMENT",0))
	Twarranty=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"WARRANTY",0))
	TDelivery=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"DELIVERY",0))

	if trim(Tvalidity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+4.5*satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tvalidity)
	Else
		if trim(validity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &validity)
		end if
	end if
if trim(tdelivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tdelivery)
	Else
		if trim(delivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tdelivery)
		end if
	end if

	if trim(tpayment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tPayment)
	else
		if trim(payment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Payment)
		end if
	end if
	
	if trim(twarranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &twarranty)
	else
		if trim(warranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &warranty)
		end if

	end if
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 6)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("center")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Beytepe Mah.1725. No: 12 06800 Beytepe / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	
			res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(TEKL40T.PRICEUNIT(1))

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA #"
	Grid(1,2) = "KATALOG NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "ADET"
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 76
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, sira
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar, solad, solmargin1
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n, toplam, iskontotoplam, geneltoplam, blok2, blok21, logo, logo1, gk1
Public xCizgi(), chkodu, sonsatir2, logo_lik, adr1, adr2, adr3

Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaeni=210
	sayfiskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0

	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next

	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****logo
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))

					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))
		End Select
		res=pe1.nopen()

		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)

		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")
		FAX=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kontakt, "")
		adr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		adr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_3", kontakt, "")


		kontaktpozisyon=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kontakt, "")
		kontaktpozisyon_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",kontaktpozisyon,-1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHtel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHfax = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		

		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		satirara=5
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))



		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 6,100, chadi)
	
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)

		Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
		EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)
 
		QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
		Set Old1 = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
		
satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom
	
		For  c = 1 To old1.getreccount
			Mesaj = Trim(Old1.NOTES(c))
			Mesaj1 = mesaj1 & mesaj

		Next
					mesaj1=replace(trim(mesaj1),"-"," ") 
		k=len(mesaj1)/100
if k < 1 then
		k=1
	end if

	res=pe1.setalign("justified")

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5*k,SagMargin - solmargin1-10 ,Mesaj1)	
			
			satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom

		


		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiAlan_ad = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","AD",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		NAME2 = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","NAME2",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		EMAIL = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","EMAIL",SiparisiAlan,"") 

		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,"Saygýlarýmýzla,")		
		satirpozr1=satirpozr1+satiryuksekligi*3

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,siparisialan_AD)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,naME2)		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1+Satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin1 , 5,SagMargin - solmargin1+10 ,EMAIL)	
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube:Beytepe Mah. 1725. Sok. No:12 06800 Beytepe / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")




		res=pe1.pagebreak
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		solmargin=10

				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res=pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Solmargin, 30,sagmargin-solmargin-10,logo_Lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
'*******************

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		TeklifNo = TEKL40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		ProjeAdi = TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Sadres1=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_1
		Sadres2=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_2
		Sadres3=TEKL40E.SEVKADRESI_3
		SAD=TEKL40E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = TEKL40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+37.5
		SatirAra = 5

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,chadi)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adr3)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"From")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 28, 6,85,":  " & Siparisialan)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		'res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 2, 6,26,"To")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5 * SatirAra, solmargin + 28, 6,30,":  " & trim(Siparisiveren))

		

			Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,43,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)


' Sütun basliklari
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 9, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		Not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(Satirno)
		
		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1, satirno)
		End If
if trim(not1) <> "" then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If
		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				if trim(not1) <> "" then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
					Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				elseif degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=TEKL40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.TL_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.TL_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom
End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
if pe1.getbottom+20 > satirsonu then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+1, SolMargin+50 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

end sub

Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, satirno)
	Ondalik = 2
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
if satirno<> 1 then

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
		isk=Doc.div(iskontotoplam, toplam)*100

	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO %"&doc.numformat_nlz(isk,10,2))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
end if
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPSÝYONLAR")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_Toplam_Yazdir (pe1)
	Ondalik = 2
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
		isk=Doc.div(iskontotoplam, toplam)*100

	TOPLAM=0
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO %"&doc.numformat_nlz(isk,10,2))

		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTOP ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	'chkodu1=Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",gk1,-1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "GENEL TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "Fiyatlarýmýza KDV Dahil Deðildir.")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, doc.numtotext(TEKL40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM))
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirbaslangic + satiryuksekligi*7
'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
	'	res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, sonsatir2-(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3), 0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, cizgiboyu, 0)

	Next
'dis çerçeve
	

	res = pe1.box(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin, cizgiboyu, SagMargin - SolMargin)


' notlar mesajlar
	Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
	EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)

	QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
	Set Old = Doc.Select1("_GNMESGGG", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 

		satirpozr=satirsonu
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)

	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	end if

		

	Tvalidity=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"VALIDITY",0))
	Tpayment=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"PAYMENT",0))
	Twarranty=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"WARRANTY",0))
	TDelivery=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"DELIVERY",0))
	if trim(Tvalidity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+4.5*satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tvalidity)
	Else
		if trim(validity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &validity)
		end if
	end if
if trim(tdelivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tdelivery)
	Else
		if trim(delivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tdelivery)
		end if
	end if

	if trim(tpayment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tPayment)
	else
		if trim(payment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Payment)
		end if
	end if
	
	if trim(twarranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &twarranty)
	else
		if trim(warranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &warranty)
		end if

	end if
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 6)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("center")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Boðaz Sokak No:30/6 06700 Kavaklýdere-Gaziosmanpaþa / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	
			res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(DOC.DEFAULTPB)

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "TL_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA #"
	Grid(1,2) = "KATALOG NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "ADET"
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 76
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	iskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0

	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20
	iskontotoplam=0
	toplam=0
	geneltoplam=0

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next

	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****logo
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))

					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))
		End Select
		res=pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		kontakt=TEKL40E.CNT_KOD
		kontaktisim=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kontakt, "")
		TEL=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kontakt, "")
FAX=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kontakt, "")


		kontaktpozisyon=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kontakt, "")
		kontaktpozisyon_adi=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",kontaktpozisyon,-1)
		adr1=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_1", kontakt, "")
		adr2=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_2", kontakt, "")
		adr3=doc.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_ADR_3", kontakt, "")


		CHKodu = TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHtel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHfax = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL40E.TARIH(1))
		Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24

		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		satirara=5
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))



		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi, SolMargin1, 6,100, chadi)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*2, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr1)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr2)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*4, SolMargin1, 6,100, adr3)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*5, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Telefon : "& tel)
		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*6, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Faks : "& fax)

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)
		Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
		EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)
 
		QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
		Set Old1 = Doc.Select1("KKfdgsdgf", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 
		
		satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom
	
		For  c = 1 To old1.getreccount
			Mesaj = Trim(Old1.NOTES(c))
			Mesaj1 = mesaj1 & mesaj

		Next
					mesaj1=replace(trim(mesaj1),"-"," ") 
		k=len(mesaj1)/100
if k < 1 then
		k=1
	end if

	res=pe1.setalign("justified")

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5*k,SagMargin - solmargin1-10 ,Mesaj1)	
			
			satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom

		

		SiparisiAlan = TEKL40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		SiparisiAlan_ad = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","AD",SiparisiAlan,"") 
		NAME2 = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","NAME2",SiparisiAlan,"")

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		Not1=TEKL40T.NOTES(Satirno)
		
		refnum1 = TEKL40T.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(satirno)
		if refnum1 > 0 then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir(pe1, satirno)
		End If
if trim(not1) <> "" then
			Call Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
		End If
		Dim satirpozr
		If trim(TEKL40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+5
		Else
				If satirNo = 1 Then
				if trim(not1) <> "" then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+8
				else
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic+2
				end if
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.gettop + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If	
				
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
					Degisken = trim(cstr(TEKL40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				If degiskenadi="_SIRANO" then
					sira=sira+1
					degisken=sira
				elseif degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=TEKL40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=TEKL40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
							Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,0)
						else
									Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,2)
						end if
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "C" Then
						if degiskenadi<>"KOD_STOK00_AD" then
									res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
						else
									res = pe1.SetAlign("left")
						end if

					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				if degiskenadi="TL_TUTAR" then
					soltutar = sol
					gentutar = sutungenisligi- 4
				ElseIf degiskenadi="KOD_STOK00_AD" then
					solad = sol
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

		toplam = toplam + TEKL40T.TL_TUTAR(satirno)
		iskontotoplam = iskontotoplam + TEKL40T.TL_XISKONTO_TUTARI(satirno)
		geneltoplam = geneltoplam + TEKL40T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(satirno)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if satirno=TEKL40T.getreccount then
			'res=pe1.line(pe1.getbottom, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		end if
			Sonsatir2=pe1.getbottom
End Sub
Sub Print_AraToplam_yazdir1(pe1, not1, satirno)
if pe1.getbottom+20 > satirsonu then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+1, SolMargin+50 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

end sub

Sub Print_AraToplam_Yazdir (pe1, not1, satirno)
	Ondalik = 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
if satirno<> 1 then

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, Solad , satiryuksekligi ,30,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(toplam, 20, ondalik))
	TOPLAM=0
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	If Iskontotoplam > 0 then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(Iskontotoplam, 20, ondalik))
		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	geneltoplam=0
end if

if pe1.getbottom+5 > satirsonu then
	CALL Print_form_sayfasonu(PE1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirpozr = satirBaslangic
end if


	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(not1)="" then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, 60 , satiryuksekligi ,70,"OPSÝYONLAR")
	else
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi, 60 , satiryuksekligi ,70,not1)

	end if
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

End Sub

	Ondalik = 2

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom ,Solad, satiryuksekligi ,40, "ÝSKONTO")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

		iskontotoplaM=0
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,40, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.gettop ,Soltutar , satiryuksekligi ,gentutar, doc.numformat(geneltoplam, 20, ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	'chkodu1=Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",gk1,-1)

	odeme=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",chkodu1,"ODEME",0))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, "Fiyatlarýmýza KDV Dahil Deðildir.")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+Satiryuksekligi , Solad , satiryuksekligi ,100, doc.numtotext(TEKL40E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM))
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'notlarla toplam arasindaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirbaslangic + satiryuksekligi*7
'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
	'	res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, sonsatir2-(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3), 0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, LineX, cizgiboyu, 0)

	Next
'dis çerçeve
	

	res = pe1.box(satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi*3, SolMargin, cizgiboyu, SagMargin - SolMargin)


' notlar mesajlar
	Tablo = "TEKL40E" 
	EvrakKartNo = trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO)

	QStr = "TABLE1='"+Tablo+"'&KRITER='"+EvrakKartNo+"'" 
	Set Old = Doc.Select1("_GNMESGGG", "GNMESG2", QStr, "", -1) 

		satirpozr=satirsonu
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)

	end if
	if trim(TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)<> "" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,150,TEKL40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	end if

	'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next
	
	Tvalidity=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"VALIDITY",0))
	Tpayment=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"PAYMENT",0))
	Twarranty=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"WARRANTY",0))
	TDelivery=CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL40E",TEKL40E.EVRAKNO,"DELIVERY",0))

	if trim(Tvalidity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+4.5*satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tvalidity)
	Else
		if trim(validity)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Geçerlilik")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &validity)
		end if
	end if
if trim(tdelivery)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &Tdelivery)
	Else

		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Teslimat")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tdelivery)
		end if
	end if

	if trim(tpayment)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Ödeme Þekli")
		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &tPayment)

		res=pe1.setbold(1)

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

		end if
	end if
	
	if trim(twarranty)<>"" then
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satiryuksekligi
		res=pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin , 5,50,"Garanti")

		res=pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,solmargin+35 , 5,150,":" &warranty)
		end if

	end if
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 6)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("center")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("center")

				res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(altmargin ,solmargin , 5,180,"Merkez: Yukarý Dudullu Mah. Tavukçuyolu Cad. No:243 34775 Ümraniye-ÝSTANBUL Tel: 0216 508 24 06 Fax: 0216 508 24 11")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")
		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi*2 ,solmargin , 5,180,"Þube: Boðaz Sokak No:30/6 06700 Kavaklýdere-Gaziosmanpaþa / ANKARA Tel: 0312 468 08 53 Fax: 0312 468 08 59")



		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	
			res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	PB = trim(doc.defaultpb)

	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "TL_TUTAR"
	

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA #"
	Grid(1,2) = "KATALOG NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "ADET"
	Grid(4,2) = "BÝRÝM"&" "&PB
	Grid(5,2) = "TUTAR"&" "&PB
	

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 76
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 25

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = TEKL40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_ISKONTOLU_FIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	Bbasla=ustmargin-1
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
				res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)

	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			if i=1 then
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			else
				res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok2, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			end if
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 13 * satiryuksekligi, blok21, 6,20,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, sira
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar, solad, solmargin1
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis, CHKODU1
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n, toplam, iskontotoplam, geneltoplam, blok2, blok21, logo, logo1, gk1
Public xCizgi(), chkodu, sonsatir2, adr1, adr2, adr3, Eadr1, Eadr2, Eadr3
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	iskontotoplam=0
	geneltoplam=0
	toplam=0
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	sira = 0
	soltutar = 0
	gentutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.5
	Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
	Blok21 = Blok2 + 20

	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + satiryuksekligi*19
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 25 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0
	gk1 = mid(trim(TEKL40E.FIYATLISTESI),1, 2)
	if trim(gk1)="" then
			msgbox "Fiyat Listesi Seçmelisiniz"
			exit sub
		end if
'******CH OKU
		set cari = Doc.Select1("ABCxxxxx","CARI00","TEXT_TECH_1='"+GK1+"'","",-1)	
		for d=1 to cari.getreccount
			chkodu1=cari.KOD(D)
		next
	ReDim xCizgi(1)

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Teklifi",  SayfaBoyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = TEKL40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_onyazi(pe1)

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_Toplam_Yazdir(pe1)
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call sayfasayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_onyazi(pe1)
		solmargin1=20

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res=pe1.nopen
		logo_lik = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'*****LOGO OKU
	
		
		Select Case trim(gk1)
					Case "WA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W"))
						logo1 = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_W1"))
res=pe1.nopen
					Case "HR"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_HR"))
					Case "TA"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_TA"))
					Case "EV"
						logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO_EV"))

		End Select
res=pe1.nopen
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Sagmargin - 70, 30,70,logo_lik,114)

		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1)

		Blok2 = SolMargin + 130
		Blok21 = Blok2 + 24
		
			
		basla9 = ustmargin + 80
		satirara=5
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 6.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,20,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok2, 6,33,"Proforma No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla9 - 5.5 * SatirAra, Blok21, 6,25,": " & trim(TEKL40E.EVRAKNO))


		res = pe1.Write(basla9, SolMargin1, 6,100,  kontaktisim)

		res = pe1.Write(basla9 + satiryuksekligi*9, SolMargin1, 6,100, "Sayýn "& kontaktisim)

		Tablo = "TEKL40E"

		Next
					mesaj1=replace(trim(mesaj1),"-"," ")

	if k < 1 then
		k=1
		end if
	k=Doc.Ceil(k)
	res=pe1.setalign("justified")

		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr1,solmargin1 , 5*k,SagMargin - solmargin1-10 ,Mesaj1)	
			
			satirpozr1=pe1.getbottom

		EMAIL = doc.Getdbfieldvalue("PERS00","EMAIL",SiparisiAlan,"")

		Res = pe1.Write(altmargin+satiryuksekligi ,solmargin , 5,180,"e-mail: satis@likrom.com,  e-mail:servis@likrom.com,  http:// www.likrom.com ")

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
