Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	soltutar=0
	gentutar=0
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 67
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 15 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  Sayfaeni, Sayfaboyu,"landscape")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin+2,SolMargin+85,8,100,"LÝKROM FATURA TALEP FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 12
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra+2
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 2, 6,100,"Firma Ünvaný :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,cHadi)
		
		
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(Adres1) & " " & trim(adres2) &" "&trim(adres3))
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi D. ve No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,30,"Sipariþ No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþ Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Ýlgili Kiþi/Dept.,Tel,Fax:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		kkod=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "CNT_KOD", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")

		kAD=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kkod, "")
		kdep=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kkod, "")
		KDEPAD=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",KDEP,-1)
		kTEL=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kkod, "")
		kFAX=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kkod, "")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(kad)& " / "&trim(kdepAD)&" / "&trim(ktel)&" / "&trim(kfax))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Likrom Prof.No.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Müþteri Ref.No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)


'		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.CHSIPNO)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,80,Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"MUSST",0)))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Teklif Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "TARIH", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,40,"Müþt.Sipariþ Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"MUST",0) ))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+6,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+12,solmargin+48,0,sagmargin-solmargin-48)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+18,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+24,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+36,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)		
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+48,45,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+184,8,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin+184,13,0)	
		
		
' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
				SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+10, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+55, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(satirbaslangic-2, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)

		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=stok40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						ondalik=2
					else
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					end if
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
					End If
				Else
'mmm
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					End If
				End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						soltutar=sol
						gentutar=sutungenisligi-2
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
'		res=pe1.line(satirpozr, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2, sagmargin-solmargin)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12

	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2+47, sagmargin-solmargin)
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res=pe1.line(satirsonu, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)


	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	Not3 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3(1)
	Not4 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4(1)

	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	
	satirbitis=satirsonu+2
	SatirYuksekligi = 6
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,soltutar-30,6,30,"Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"Ýndirim %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Ara  Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Genel Toplam")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("right")

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis ,soltutar,6,gentutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, IskontoToplami)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,12,gentutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	satiryuksekligi=5
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sevk Adresi :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+2,6,30,"Notlar :")

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+30,6,70,sevkAdresi1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+30,6,125,not1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*6,solmargin+2,6,125,not2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*7,solmargin+2,6,125,not3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*8,solmargin+2,6,125,not4)


	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sipariþi Açan :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+2,6,30,"Vade Tarihi :")

	alan = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+30,6,30,alan)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+30,6,30,Odeme1)





End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	
	

	Grid(0,1) = 48
	Grid(1,1) = 102
	Grid(2,1) = 22
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 35



	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Numarasý"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklamalar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Toplam Tutar"

	

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"


	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, cizgiboyu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, soltutar, gentutar
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla, sevkAdresi1, sevkAdresi2, sevkAdresi3
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	soltutar=0
	gentutar=0
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 67
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 15 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  Sayfaeni, Sayfaboyu,"landscape")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	'Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin+2,SolMargin+85,8,100,"LÝKROM SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))
		res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
'		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 12
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra+2
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 2, 6,100,"Firma Ünvaný :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,cHadi)
		
		
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(Adres1) & " " & trim(adres2) &" "&trim(adres3))
		satirpozr=satirpozr+satirara
			

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi D. ve No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr+1 , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,30,"Sipariþ No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþ Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Ýlgili Kiþi/Dept.,Tel,Fax:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		kkod=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "CNT_KOD", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")

		kAD=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "AD_SOYAD", kkod, "")
		kdep=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "POZISYON", kkod, "")
		KDEPAD=Doc.GetGecoValue("MESUNV","AD",KDEP,-1)
		kTEL=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "IS_TELEFONU", kkod, "")
		kFAX=DOC.getdbfieldvalue("KONTAKT0", "SAHSI_FAX", kkod, "")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,140,trim(kad)& " / "&trim(kdepAD)&" / "&trim(ktel)&" / "&trim(kfax))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Likrom Prof.No.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Müþteri Ref.No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.CHSIPNO)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Teklif Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(DOC.getdbfieldvalue("TEKL40E", "TARIH", STOK40T.TEKLIFNO(1), "")))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,40,"Müþt.Sipariþ Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Doc.strtonordate(doc.GetUDFValue("STOK40E",STOK40E.EVRAKNO,"MUST",0) ))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+6,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+12,solmargin+48,0,sagmargin-solmargin-48)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+18,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+24,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+36,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)		
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+48,45,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+184,8,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin+184,13,0)	
		
		
' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
				SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+10, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(ustmargin+55, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
		res=pe1.line(satirbaslangic-2, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)

		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				if degiskenadi="KOD" then

					stokkodu=stok40T.KOD(satirno)
					chkodu=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU
					if trim(DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))<> "" then
						degisken=DOC.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
					end if

				End If

					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						ondalik=2
					else
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					end if
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
					End If
				Else
'mmm
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					End If
				End If
				if degiskenadi="OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" then
						soltutar=sol
						gentutar=sutungenisligi-2
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
'		res=pe1.line(satirpozr, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2, sagmargin-solmargin)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	cizgiBoyu = Satirsonu - satirBaslangic+12

	res=pe1.box(ustmargin-2, solmargin, satirsonu-ustmargin+2+47, sagmargin-solmargin)
	for k=1 to 15
			res=pe1.box(satirbaslangic+k*satiryuksekligi, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)
	next

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res=pe1.line(satirsonu, solmargin, 0, sagmargin-solmargin)


	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	Not3 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3(1)
	Not4 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4(1)

	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	
	satirbitis=satirsonu+2
	SatirYuksekligi = 6
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,soltutar-30,6,30,"Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"Ýndirim %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Ara  Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar-30,6,30,"Genel Toplam")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("right")

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis ,soltutar,6,gentutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, IskontoToplami)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 2 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 3 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,12,gentutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis  + 4 * satirYuksekligi,soltutar,6,gentutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	satiryuksekligi=5
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sevk Adresi :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+2,6,30,"Notlar :")

		res=pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis,solmargin+30,6,70,sevkAdresi1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*2,solmargin+2,6,70,sevkAdresi3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*5,solmargin+30,6,70,not1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*6,solmargin+2,6,70,not2)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*7,solmargin+2,6,70,not3)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*8,solmargin+2,6,70,not4)
	
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+2,6,30,"Sipariþi Açan :")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+2,6,30,"Vade Tarihi :")

	alan = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*3,solmargin+30,6,30,alan)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+satiryuksekligi*4,solmargin+30,6,30,Odeme1)






End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	
	

	Grid(0,1) = 48
	Grid(1,1) = 102
	Grid(2,1) = 22
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 35



	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Numarasý"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklamalar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Toplam Tutar"

	

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"


	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 67
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin+2,SolMargin+85,8,100,"LÝKROM SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.2)
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))

		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 12
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra+2
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 2, 6,100,"Firma Ünvaný :")
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,cHadi)
		
		If trim(cHadi2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 40, 6,100,cHadi2)
		End If
		If trim(Adres1) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra+1
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,100,trim(Adres1) & " " & trim(adres2) & " " & trim(adres3))
		End If
		If trim(Adres2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra+1
			'res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,100,Adres2)
		End If

		If trim(Adres3) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra+1
			'res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 50, 6,100,Adres3)
		End If
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi D. ve No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr , SolMargin + 50, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,30,"Sipariþ No :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþ Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Ýlgili Kiþi/Dept.,Tel,Fax:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4.8 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Likrom Prof.No.")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Müþteri Ref.No")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 50, 6,70,STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Teklif Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 5.9 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,40,"Müþt.Sipariþ Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 7.1 *SatirAra, SolMargin + 225, 6,20,STOK40E.NAKLIYECIFIRMA)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+6,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+12,solmargin+48,0,sagmargin-solmargin-48)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+18,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+24,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.2)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+36,solmargin,0,sagmargin-solmargin)		
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+48,60,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla-2,solmargin+184,8,0)	
		res = pe1.line(BBasla+30,solmargin+184,13,0)	
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+135,solmargin+184,31,0)	
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+135,solmargin+229,31,0)	
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+142,solmargin+184,0,93)
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+148,solmargin+184,0,93)
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+154,solmargin+184,0,93)
		res = pe1.line(ustmargin+160,solmargin+184,0,93)

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+155,solmargin+2,6,50,"Sipariþi Veren")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+160,solmargin+2,6,50,STOK40E.SIPIVEREN)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+155,solmargin+150,6,50,"Onay :")
' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
				SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		n = n + 1
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
					End If
				Else
'mmm
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					End If
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)

'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	
	res = pe1.box(SatirBaslangic - 10, SolMargin, satirBitis - SatirBaslangic + 10, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - UstMargin - 47.5
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
'yatay çizgi
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		If i <> 3 And i <> 5 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+47.5, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.box(ustmargin, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic + 66, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+10, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 12, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic-20, SagMargin - SolMargin)


'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic -20
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
		If i <> 2 And i <> 4  and i<> 6 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.2)	
	for i= 1 to 11
		res = pe1.line(SatirBaslangic +i*5.5,solmargin,0,SagMargin - SolMargin)
	next
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)


	Blok2 = SolMargin + 35
	Genislik = xCizgi(4) - Blok2 - 2		
	satirpozr = SatirBitis + 2

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	
	
		
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom() + 2

	Ondalik = Grid(7,6)
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	
	sss = SatirYuksekligi

	SatirYuksekligi = 6

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30,solmargin+188,6,30,"Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + satirYuksekligi,solmargin+188,6,40,"Ýndirim %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+188,6,30,"Ara  Toplam")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+188,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+188,6,30,"Genel Toplam")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

	lll = xCizgi(8)
	xCizgi(8) = xCizgi(7)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Gen = xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6) - 4
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30,solmargin+220,6,50, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + satirYuksekligi,solmargin+220,6,50, IskontoToplami)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+220,6,50, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+220,12,50, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis -30 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solmargin+220,6,50, KDVDahilTutar)


	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
'ppp
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	
	

	Grid(0,1) = 48
	Grid(1,1) = 102
	Grid(2,1) = 22
	Grid(3,1) = 12
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 35



	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Numarasý"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklamalar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Toplam Tutar"

	

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"


	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu,sayfaEni, satirSonu,sonSatir, UstMargin, pe1

Public MailPDF,FirmaHaberVerilecekListesine,MailSablonNo

MailSablonNo=" "
MailPDF=FALSE
FirmaHaberVerilecekListesine=FALSE
Public SRVM00E
Public SRVM00T
Set SRVM00E=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVM00E")
Set SRVM00T=Doc.GetTableObject("SRVM00T")

Sub PrintForm()
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 15
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	basla1=ustmargin+32
	basla2=basla1+42
	basla3=basla2+30
	basla4=basla3+31
	basla5=basla4+71
	basla6=basla5+25
	yapilanis=""
	satirBaslangic = basla3+satiryuksekligi+1
	satirpozr=satirbaslangic-satiryuksekligi

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "SevisFormu - " & trim(SRVM00E.MUSTERIKODU) & "," & trim(SRVM00E.EVRAKNO),  Sayfaboyu, Sayfaeni,"")
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0   
	satirSayisi = SRVM00T.GetRecCount()     
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For z = 1 To satirSayisi
			yapilanis = yapilanis & " " & trim(SRVM00T.YAPILANISLEM_TEXT(z))
	next
	
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	if Not MailPDF then
		res = pe1.Show()
		res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
	end if
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		logo = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO1")
		logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & logo

		res = pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin,Solmargin, 15,sagmargin-solmargin,logo,114)
		servisno=SRVM00E.EVRAKNO(1)
		
		res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)
		res = pe1.Barcode(Ustmargin,solmargin+145,15,30,"CODE39", trim(servisno),"")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 13)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
		res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(0,0,0))
		res = pe1.box(ustmargin+20,solmargin,10,185)
		res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
		res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))
		res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
		res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(255,255,255))	
		res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin+22, solmargin-5, 15, sagmargin - solamargin,"SERVÝS / ÝÞ EMRÝ HÝZMET FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(0,0,0))		
		
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

		musteri=SRVM00E.UNVAN1
		adres1=SRVM00E.ADRES_1
		adres2=SRVM00E.ADRES_2
		adres3=SRVM00E.ADRES_3
		yetkili=SRVM00E.KARSILAYANFRMGOREVLI
		tel=SRVM00E.FIRMA_TELEFON
		fax=SRVM00E.FIRMA_FAXNO

		servisno=SRVM00E.EVRAKNO
		tarih=doc.strtonordate(SRVM00E.TARIH)
		cagriyialan=SRVM00E.OPERATORKODU_AD

		talepeden=SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLI
		taleptarih=doc.strtonordate(SRVM00T.SOZVERILEN_TARIH(1))
		cagrinotu=SRVM00E.CARGRINOTU(1)
		yapilmasiistenen=SRVM00T.YPLMSISTENEN_TEXT(1)
		
		
'*********basla1
		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+1, 15, 20,"Müþteri")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 15, 20,"Adres")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+4*satiryuksekligi , solmargin+1, 15, 20,"Yetkili")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+5*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 15, 30,"Tel / Fax")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+20, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+20, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+4*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+20, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+5*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+20, 15, 2,":")	

	

		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+95, 15, 30,"Servis No")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+95, 15, 30,"Çaðrý Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+2*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+95, 15, 30,"Çaðrýyý Alan")
		
		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+120, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+120, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+2*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+120, 15, 2,":")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)

		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+22, 15, 80,trim(musteri))
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+22, 15,80 ,trim(adres1))
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+2*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 15,80,trim(adres2))
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+3*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 15,80,trim(adres3))
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+4*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+22, 15, 80,trim(yetkili))	
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+5*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+22, 15, 80,trim(tel) & " / " & fax)

		res = pe1.Write(basla1, solmargin+122, 15, 30,trim(servisno))
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+122, 15, 30,tarih)
		res = pe1.Write(basla1+2*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+122, 15,30,trim(cagriyialan))

'************basla2
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+1, 15, 50,"Talep Eden Kiþi")

		res = pe1.Write(basla2+3*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 15, 50,"Yapýlmasý Ýstenen")
		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+42, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla2+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+42, 15, 2,":")
		res = pe1.Write(basla2+3*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+42, 15, 2,":")

		

		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+95, 15, 50,"Talep Edilen Tarih")
		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+127, 15, 2,":")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+44, 15,30,trim(talepeden))
		res = pe1.Write(basla2+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+44, 15, 150,Trim(cagrinotu))
		res = pe1.Write(basla2+3*satiryuksekligi, solmargin+44, 15, 100,yapilmasiistenen)
		res = pe1.Write(basla2, solmargin+129, 15, 30,taleptarih)

'************basla3

'*************basla5

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)

		If trim(SRVM00T.OPERATORKODU(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		
		satirpozr = satirpozr + satiryuksekligi
						
			

		kisi1=SRVM00T.OPERATORKODU(satirno)
		kisi=doc.getdbfieldvalue("SRVMP0","ADSOYAD",kisi1,"")
		bastarih=doc.strtonordate(SRVM00T.RECTARIH1(satirno))
		bassaat=SRVM00T.RECTIME1(satirno)
		bitsaat=SRVM00T.RECTIME2(satirno)
		gun=SRVM00T.SURE_GUN(satirno)
		saat=SRVM00T.SURE(satirno)
		onay=SRVM00T.MUSTERIONAYINIVEREN(satirno)

		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr , solmargin+1, 15, 50,kisi)	
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr , solmargin+100, 15, 50,bastarih)
		if trim(bassaat)="" then

		else	
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr , solmargin+130, 15, 60,trim(bassaat) & " / " & trim(bitsaat) & " - " & trim(gun) & " Gün " & " / "  & trim(saat) & " Saat")	
		end if
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr+21.5*satiryuksekligi , solmargin+5, 10, 40,trim(onay))
		

	

end sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

		res = pe1.Write(basla4+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+1, 60, 150,trim(yapilanis))


		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

		res = pe1.box(basla1-2,solmargin,37,185)
		res = pe1.box(basla2-2,solmargin,25,185)
		res = pe1.box(basla3-2,solmargin,26,185)
		res = pe1.box(basla4-2,solmargin,65,185)	
		res = pe1.box(basla5-2,solmargin,20,185)	
		res = pe1.box(basla6-2,solmargin,25,185)
		

		res = pe1.line(basla3+6,solmargin,0,185)
		res = pe1.line(basla3+12,solmargin,0,185)
		res = pe1.line(basla3+18,solmargin,0,185)
		res = pe1.line(basla3-2,solmargin+99,26,0)
		res = pe1.line(basla3-2,solmargin+129,26,0)

End Sub
Sub PrintForm_MailPDF()

	if Trim(MailSablonNo)="" then
		MailSablonNo="1"
	end if
	
	MailPDF=TRUE
	Call PrintForm()

	Dim i
	Dim OperatorMail
	Dim MailListesi
	for i=1 to SRVM00T.GetRecCount()
		if Trim(SRVM00T.OPERATORKODU(i))<>"" then
			OperatorMail=Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SRVMP0","EMAIL",SRVM00T.OPERATORKODU(i)," ")
			if Trim(OperatorMail)<>"" then				
				if Trim(MailListesi)<>"" then
					MailListesi=MailListesi+";"
				end if
				MailListesi=MailListesi+Trim(OperatorMail)
			end if
		end if
	next

	if Trim(SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLIMAIL)<>"" then
		if Trim(MailListesi)<>"" then
			MailListesi=MailListesi+";"
		end if
		MailListesi=MailListesi+Trim(SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLIMAIL)+";"
	end if

	if FirmaHaberVerilecekListesine then
		if Trim(SRVM00E.MUSTERIKODU)<>"" then
			Dim TABL76T
			Set TABL76T=Doc.Select1("_TABL76T_123123HHJJUU","TABL76T","AKTIF_PASIF='*' & FIRMAKODU='"+SRVM00E.MUSTERIKODU+"'","SRNUM",-1) 
			if Not Doc.IsBlank(TABL76T) then
				if TABL76T.GetRecCount()>0 then
					for i=1 to TABL76T.GetRecCount()
						if Trim(TABL76T.HABERVERILECEKGRVLEM(i))<>"" and Trim(TABL76T.HABERVERILECEKGRVLEM(i))<>Trim(SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLIMAIL) then
							if Trim(MailListesi)<>"" then
								MailListesi=MailListesi+";"
							end if
							MailListesi=MailListesi+Trim(TABL76T.HABERVERILECEKGRVLEM(i))+";"
						end if
					next
				end if
			end if
		end if
	end if

	if Trim(RePlace(MailListesi,";"," "))="" then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Gönderilecek Mail Adresi Bulunamadý. Ýþlem Ýptal...","dinamo",MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if

	Dim TABL64E
	Dim M_TO
	Dim M_CC
	Dim HITAP
	Dim MESAJ
	Dim KAPANIS_HITAP
	Dim KAPANIS_FIRMA_BILGI
	Dim M_DIPNOT
	Dim M_ATTACH
	Dim M_ATTACH2
	Dim M_ATTACH3
	Dim M_ATTACH4
'	Set TABL64E=Doc.Select1("_TABL64E_1231HHYYUU","TABL64E","AKTIF_PASIF='*' & KULLANIMYERI='0014'","",-1)
	Set TABL64E=Doc.Select1("_TABL64E_1231HHYYUU","TABL64E","KULLANIMYERI='0014' & EVRAKNO='"+MailSablonNo+"'","",-1)
	MailSablonNo=" "
	if Not Doc.IsBlank(TABL64E) then
		if TABL64E.GetRecCount()>0 then
			M_TO=Trim(TABL64E.M_TO(1))
			M_CC=Trim(TABL64E.M_CC(1))
			HITAP=Trim(TABL64E.HITAP(1))
			MESAJ=Trim(TABL64E.MESAJ(1))
			KAPANIS_HITAP=Trim(TABL64E.KAPANIS_HITAP(1))
			KAPANIS_FIRMA_BILGI=Trim(TABL64E.KAPANIS_FIRMA_BILGI(1))
			M_DIPNOT=Trim(TABL64E.M_DIPNOT(1))
			M_ATTACH=Trim(TABL64E.M_ATTACH(1))
			M_ATTACH2=Trim(TABL64E.M_ATTACH2(1))
			M_ATTACH3=Trim(TABL64E.M_ATTACH3(1))
			M_ATTACH4=Trim(TABL64E.M_ATTACH4(1))
		end if
	end if

	Call RePlaceFieldsValues(M_TO)
	Call RePlaceFieldsValues(M_CC)
	Call RePlaceFieldsValues(HITAP)
	if Trim(M_TO)<>"" then
		if Trim(MailListesi)<>"" then
			MailListesi=MailListesi+";"
		end if
		MailListesi=MailListesi+M_TO+";"
	end if

	if KAPANIS_HITAP<>"" then
		MESAJ=MESAJ+vbCrLf+vbCrLf
		MESAJ=MESAJ+KAPANIS_HITAP
	end if
	if KAPANIS_FIRMA_BILGI<>"" then
		MESAJ=MESAJ+vbCrLf+vbCrLf
		MESAJ=MESAJ+KAPANIS_FIRMA_BILGI
	end if
	if M_DIPNOT<>"" then
		MESAJ=MESAJ+vbCrLf+vbCrLf
		MESAJ=MESAJ+M_DIPNOT
	end if
	
	Call RePlaceFieldsValues(MESAJ)

	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.SetMailSubject(HITAP)
	Dim MailListDim
	MailListesi=RePlace(MailListesi,",",";")
	MailListDim=Split(MailListesi,";")
	for i=0 to uBound(MailListDim)
		if Trim(MailListDim(i))<>"" then
			res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(Trim(MailListDim(i)),1)
		end if
	next
	if M_CC<>"" then
		M_CC=RePlace(M_CC,",",";")
		MailListDim=Split(M_CC,";")		
		for i=0 to uBound(MailListDim)
			if Trim(MailListDim(i))<>"" then
				res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(Trim(MailListDim(i)),2)
			end if
		next
	end if
	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment("",0)
	Dim AtCounter
	AtCounter=0
	if Trim(M_ATTACH)<>"" then 
		AtCounter=AtCounter+1
		res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(Trim(M_ATTACH),AtCounter)
	end if
	if Trim(M_ATTACH2)<>"" then 
		AtCounter=AtCounter+1
		res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(Trim(M_ATTACH2),AtCounter)
	end if
	if Trim(M_ATTACH3)<>"" then 
		AtCounter=AtCounter+1
		res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(Trim(M_ATTACH3),AtCounter)
	end if
	if Trim(M_ATTACH4)<>"" then 
		AtCounter=AtCounter+1
		res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(Trim(M_ATTACH4),AtCounter)
	end if

	res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)
	res = pe1.SetMailText(MESAJ)	

	res = pe1.Maildoc()
	res = pe1.Close()

End Sub
Sub PrintForm_MailPDF2()
	MailSablonNo="2"
	FirmaHaberVerilecekListesine=TRUE
	Call PrintForm_MailPDF()
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : L2013 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : SERVIS_2
Hangi Tablo için : SRVM00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Likrom Servis/Ýþ Emri Formu
Kod : 
Public ServisSatirNo,satiryuksekligi,solmargin,sagmargin, ustmargin

Sub PrintForm()   
	Dim pe1
	sayfaboyu = 297
	SayfaEni = 210
	Solmargin = 10	
	Ustmargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - Solmargin
	satirYuksekligi = 4.6
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Servis Formu",sayfaboyu,sayfaeni,"")

	ServisSatirNo = 0
	
	Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
	satirSayisi = SRVM00T.GetRecCount()

	For i=1 To satirSayisi
		Call SatirYazdir(pe1,i)
	Next	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	BitisSayfaNo = pe1.GetPageCount
	sayfaSayisi = BitisSayfaNo - BaslangicSayfaNo + 1

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)
		logo = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO1")
		logo = doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & logo
		res = pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.Picture(ustmargin+10,Solmargin+15, 17,50,logo,114)
		servisno=SRVM00E.EVRAKNO(1)
		
		res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")

		res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin, solmargin, 10, sagmargin - solmargin,"TEKNÝK SERVÝS RAPORU")

		Musteri = SRVM00E.UNVAN1
		Adres1 = SRVM00E.ADRES_1
		Adres2 = SRVM00E.ADRES_2
		Adres3 = SRVM00E.ADRES_3
		Yetkili = SRVM00E.KARSILAYANFRMGOREVLI
		Tel = SRVM00E.FIRMA_TELEFON
		Fax = SRVM00E.FIRMA_FAXNO
		Servisno = SRVM00E.EVRAKNO
		Tarih = doc.strtonordate(SRVM00E.TARIH)
		CagriyiAlan = SRVM00E.OPERATORKODU_AD
		TalepEden = SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLI
		TalepTarih = doc.strtonordate(SRVM00T.SOZVERILEN_TARIH(1))
		CagriNotu = SRVM00E.CARGRINOTU(1)
		YapilmasiIstenen = SRVM00T.YPLMSISTENEN_TEXT(1)
		Mail = SRVM00E.ARAYANFRMGOREVLIMAIL(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin+1, solmargin+130, satiryuksekligi, 30,"Rapor No")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+130, satiryuksekligi, 30," Rapor Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+69, satiryuksekligi, 30,"Müþteri / Kurum")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+69, satiryuksekligi, 30,"Departman")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+69, satiryuksekligi, 30,"Adres")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+69, satiryuksekligi, 30,"Telefon")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+69, satiryuksekligi, 30,"E-mail")

		res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin+1, solmargin+150, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+150, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+95, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+95, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+95, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+95, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+95, satiryuksekligi, 30,":")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("Right")
		res = pe1.Write(Ustmargin+1, solmargin+150, satiryuksekligi, 30,trim(ServisNo))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+150, satiryuksekligi, 30,Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Musteri))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Departman))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Adres1))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Adres2))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Telefon))
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.getbottom, solmargin+97, satiryuksekligi, 80,trim(Mail))

		res = pe1.Box(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin, satiryuksekligi*6, Sagmargin-Solmargin)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+67, satiryuksekligi*6, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*4, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*6, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*7, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*8, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		'res = pe1.Line(Ustmargin+1+satiryuksekligi*9, solmargin+67, 0, Sagmargin-Solmargin-67)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6-2, solmargin+1, satiryuksekligi, 50,"ÝSTANBUL")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6-2, solmargin+15, satiryuksekligi, 100,"Tel : 0216 368 2474 Faks : 0216 411 6795")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6+2, solmargin+1, satiryuksekligi, 50,"ANKARA")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6+2, solmargin+15, satiryuksekligi, 100,"Tel : 0312 490 4747 Faks : 0312 490 4762")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6+6, solmargin+1, satiryuksekligi, 50,"E-mail : servis@likrom.com")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin+satiryuksekligi*6+6, solmargin+38, satiryuksekligi, 50,"http://www.likrom.com")

End Sub

Sub SatirYazdir(pe1,satirNo)

	satirYuksekligi = 4.6
	iSatir = 4.6
	satirSonu = 260

	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom() + 10*iSatir

	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
	   Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		 siraNo = 1
		 res = pe1.PageBreak()
		 Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
		 satirpozr = satiryuksekligi * 11
	Else
		 'If satirNo = 1 Or ServisSatirno = 0 Then
		 If satirNo = 1 Then
			  satirpozr = satiryuksekligi * 11
		 Else
			  satirpozr = pe1.GetBottom() + satiryuksekligi
		 End If
		 siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If

		Operator = SRVM00T.OPERATORKODU(satirno)
		OperatorAdi = doc.getdbfieldvalue("SRVMP0","ADSOYAD",Operator,"")
		basTarih = doc.strtonordate(SRVM00T.RECTARIH1(satirno))
		basSaat = SRVM00T.RECTIME1(satirno)
		bitSaat = SRVM00T.RECTIME2(satirno)
		Gun = SRVM00T.SURE_GUN(satirno)
		Saat = SRVM00T.SURE(satirno)
		ServisKartNo = SRVM00T.SERVISKARTNO(satirno)
		StokKodu = SRVM00T.STOKKODU
		StokAdi = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
		SeriNo = SRVM00T.SERINO(satirno)
		Kategori1 = SRVM00T.KATEGORI1(satirno)
		YapilmasiIstenen = SRVM00T.YPLMSISTENEN(satirno)
		Garanti = SRVM00T.GARANTILIMI(satirno)
		if trim(Garanti) = "E" then
			Garanti = "Evet"
		Else 
			Garanti = "Hayýr"
		End if
		Tarih1 = SRVM00T.REF_TARIH1(satirno)
		Tarih1 = doc.strtonordate(Tarih1)
		Tarih2 = SRVM00T.REF_TARIH2(satirno)
		Tarih2 = doc.strtonordate(Tarih2)
		Tarih3 = SRVM00T.REF_TARIH3(satirno)
		Tarih3 = doc.strtonordate(Tarih3)
		Tarih4 = SRVM00T.REF_TARIH4(satirno)
		Tarih4 = doc.strtonordate(Tarih4)

		Sikayet = SRVM00T.PROBLEMISTEK_TEXT(satirno)
		Uygulama = SRVM00T.YAPILANISLEM_TEXT(satirno)

		res = pe1.box(satirpozr,solmargin,satiryuksekligi*7,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.box(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*7,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.box(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*8,solmargin,satiryuksekligi*5,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.box(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*7,solmargin+90,satiryuksekligi*6,0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Sistem Adý")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 25,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,40,"Son PM Kit/Bakým Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 27,satiryuksekligi,40,trim(Kategori1))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,solmargin + 152,satiryuksekligi,20,Tarih1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Stok Kodu")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 25,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,50,"Son Validasyon Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 27,satiryuksekligi,40,trim(StokKodu))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,solmargin + 152,satiryuksekligi,40,Tarih2)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Stok Adý ")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 25,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,50,"Bakým Söz. Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 27,satiryuksekligi,40,trim(StokAdi))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,solmargin + 152,satiryuksekligi,40,Tarih3)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Seri No")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 25,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,50,"Kurulum Tarihi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 27,satiryuksekligi,40,trim(SeriNo))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,solmargin + 152,satiryuksekligi,40,Tarih4)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Servis Tipi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 25,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,50,"Garantili mi")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 150,satiryuksekligi,5,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 27,satiryuksekligi,40,trim(YapilmasiIstenen))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,solmargin + 152,satiryuksekligi,20,trim(Garanti))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,40,"Baþlangýç Tarihi :")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 50,satiryuksekligi,40,"Bitiþ Tarihi :")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 90,satiryuksekligi,40,"Baþ. Saati :")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 125,satiryuksekligi,40,"Bitiþ Saati :")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 155,satiryuksekligi,40,"Süre (Saat) :")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 30,satiryuksekligi,20,basTarih)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 110,satiryuksekligi,20,basSaat)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 145,satiryuksekligi,20,bitSaat)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 6*iSatir,solmargin + 175,satiryuksekligi,20,Saat)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 7*iSatir,solmargin + 35,satiryuksekligi,40,"Þikayetler")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 7*iSatir,solmargin + 125,satiryuksekligi,40,"Uygulamalar")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 8*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi*5,86,trim(Sikayet))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 8*iSatir,solmargin + 92,satiryuksekligi*5,96,trim(Uygulama))

'Kullanýlan Malzemeler bölümüne baðlanma

	ArtikelNo = SRVM00T.TRNUM(satirno)
	EvrakNo = SRVM00T.EVRAKNO(satirno)	
	Dim Kriter
	Kriter = ""
	Kriter=Kriter&"EVRAKNO="&EvrakNo
	Kriter=Kriter&" & "
	Kriter=Kriter&"OR_TRNUM="&ArtikelNo

	Dim Tbl_SRVM00M
	Set Tbl_SRVM00M = Doc.Select1("__SRVM00M","SRVM00M",Kriter,"",-1)
	if Tbl_SRVM00M.Getreccount() > 0 then
		res = pe1.box(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*13,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,sagmargin-solmargin)
		res = pe1.Line(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*13,solmargin+22,satiryuksekligi,0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*13,solmargin+122,satiryuksekligi,0)
		res = pe1.Line(satirpozr+satiryuksekligi*13,solmargin+152,satiryuksekligi,0)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 13*iSatir,solmargin + 2,satiryuksekligi,20,"No")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 13*iSatir,solmargin + 30,satiryuksekligi,20,"Katalog No")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 13*iSatir,solmargin + 130,satiryuksekligi,20,"Miktar")
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 13*iSatir,solmargin + 157,satiryuksekligi,30,"Faturalanacak Mý?")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)

		For i = 1 to Tbl_SRVM00M.GetReccount()
			Hammadde = Tbl_SRVM00M.STOKKODU(i)
			HammaddeAdi = Tbl_SRVM00M.STOKKODU_AD(i)
			Miktar = Tbl_SRVM00M.MIKTAR(i)
			Fatura = Tbl_SRVM00M.FATURALANACAKMI(i)
			if trim(Fatura) = "E" then
				Fatura = "Evet"
			Else
				Fatura = "Hayýr"
			End if	
				satirpozr=pe1.getbottom
				res = pe1.Writebox(satirpozr ,solmargin,satiryuksekligi,22,i)
				res = pe1.Writebox(satirpozr ,solmargin+22,satiryuksekligi,100,trim(Hammadde) & " " &trim(HammaddeAdi))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("Right")
				res = pe1.Writebox(pe1.gettop ,solmargin+122,satiryuksekligi,30,doc.Numformat(Miktar,18,2))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("Center")
				res = pe1.Writebox(pe1.gettop ,solmargin+152,satiryuksekligi,Sagmargin-Solmargin-152,Fatura)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
		Next
	End if
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*58 ,solmargin+90,satiryuksekligi,90,"Bu raporda belirtilen süre içerisinde çalýþma tamamlanmýþ")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*59 ,solmargin+90,satiryuksekligi,95,"ve servis isteðiniz tam olarak yerine getirilmiþtir.")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)	
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*60 ,solmargin+20,satiryuksekligi,20,"TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*60 ,solmargin+80,satiryuksekligi,50,"LÝKROM SERVÝS MÜHENDÝSÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*60 ,solmargin+135,satiryuksekligi,50,"MÜÞTERÝ YETKÝLÝSÝ")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)	
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*61 ,solmargin+20,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.strtonordate(doc.today))
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*61 ,solmargin+55,satiryuksekligi,20,"Ýsim : ")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*62 ,solmargin+55,satiryuksekligi,20,"Ýmza : ")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*61 ,solmargin+135,satiryuksekligi,20,"Ýsim : ")
	res = pe1.Write(satiryuksekligi*62 ,solmargin+135,satiryuksekligi,20,"Ýmza : ")


	
End Sub
